SEIR 随机登革热流行模型的平稳分布和遍历性

Stationary distribution and ergodicity of an SEIR stochastic dengue epidemic model
作者： Yixuan Liu, Yan Zheng
摘要中文翻译论文提出了一种具有饱和发病率率的SEIR登革热流行模型,并建立了相应的随机模型。研究随机系统的动力学行为,包括全局正解的存在性和唯一性、遍历平稳分布的存在性和唯一性以及疾病灭绝的充分条件。最后,进行数值模拟来说明和支持某些理论结果的有效性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在研究 登革病毒 传播 的动态行为,特别关注随机 SEIR 模型中全局正解和遍历平稳分布的存在性和唯一性。
设计与对象
该研究利用随机 SEIR 模型来模拟 登革病毒 传播 动力学,在不指定特定人群或地理位置的情况下检查系统的数学特性。
核心方法
作者建立了具有饱和 发病率 率的随机 SEIR 模型,并通过理论研究分析了其动力学行为,包括解的存在性和唯一性以及疾病灭绝的条件。
主要结果
该研究证实了随机SEIR模型的全局正解和遍历平稳分布的存在性和唯一性,为登革热疾病的灭绝提供了充分的条件。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为有助于更深入地了解随机条件下的 登革病毒 传播 动力学,为疾病的长期行为提供新颖的理论见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可以为 登革病毒 监测 和控制开发更准确的数学模型提供信息,帮助预测疾病持续性和潜在的 暴发。
局限与证据强度
研究仅限于理论分析和数值模拟,没有关于登革病毒 传播的实证数据。证据是温和的,因为它为登革热的随机模型提供了基础见解。

Stationary distribution and ergodicity of an SEIR stochastic dengue epidemic model

Authors: Yixuan Liu, Yan Zheng
Original abstract (English)
The paper proposes an SEIR dengue epidemic model with a saturated incidence rate and establishes the corresponding stochastic model. The dynamical behavior of the stochastic system is investigated, which includes the existence and uniqueness of a global positive solution, the existence and uniqueness of an ergodic stationary distribution, and the sufficient conditions for extinction of the disease. Finally, numerical simulations are conducted to illustrate and support the effectiveness of certain theoretical results.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the dynamical behavior of dengue virus transmission, particularly focusing on the existence and uniqueness of a global positive solution and an ergodic stationary distribution in a stochastic SEIR model.
Design and population
The research utilized a stochastic SEIR model to simulate dengue virus transmission dynamics, examining the mathematical properties of the system without specifying particular populations or geographic locations.
Core methods
The authors established a stochastic SEIR model with a saturated incidence rate and analyzed its dynamical behavior through theoretical investigations, including the existence and uniqueness of solutions and the conditions for disease extinction.
Main results
The study confirmed the existence and uniqueness of a global positive solution and an ergodic stationary distribution for the stochastic SEIR model, and provided sufficient conditions for the extinction of dengue disease.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as contributing to a deeper understanding of dengue virus transmission dynamics under stochastic conditions, offering novel theoretical insights into the long-term behavior of the disease.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results can inform the development of more accurate mathematical models for dengue virus surveillance and control, aiding in the prediction of disease persistence and potential outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to theoretical analysis and numerical simulations, with no empirical data on dengue virus transmission. The evidence is moderate as it provides foundational insights into stochastic modeling of dengue.

活化部分凝血活酶时间(aPTT)作为成年登革热患者出血表现和严重程度的预测因子——一项横断面研究

Activated Partial Thromboplastin Time (aPTT) as a Predictor of Bleeding Manifestations and Severity in Adult Patients with Dengue – A Cross Sectional Study
作者： B. ThrilokRam, D. Subrahmanyam
摘要中文翻译Background: 登革热 is a leading cause of acute febrile illness in tropical regions.单独的血小板减少症与登革热出血的相关性较差。活化部分凝血活酶时间(aPTT)反映了内在的凝血功能障碍,可以作为一个更好的预测因子。Objectives: To assess deranged aPTT as a predictor of bleeding manifestations and to evaluate its association with severity of dengue in adult patients.方法:在本地治里 SVMCH & RC 进行横断面观察研究(2026 年 1 月至 3 月)。四十名经血清学证实患有登革热的成年患者被纳入研究。入院时的 aPTT 是主要暴露变量;观察患者的出血情况和 重症登革热(CDC 2015 标准)。结果:40 名患者中,20 名患者的 aPTT 延长(平均 54.8 ± 9.3 秒),20 名患者的 aPTT 正常(平均 34.6 ± 2.9 秒)。Any bleeding (RR=3.25;p=0.026),substantial bleeding (RR=3.00;p=0.048),and 重症登革热 (RR=3.75;p=0.001) were all strongly correlated with prolonged aPTT.对于显着出血,阴性预测值(NPV)为 95.0%。血小板计数和出血症状之间没有明显的关系。结论:aPTT 是一种可靠的登革热大出血和严重程度排除筛查工具,NPV 为 95%。仅血小板计数不足以预测出血风险。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热 是热带地区急性发热性疾病的主要原因。单独的血小板减少症与登革热出血的相关性较差。
设计与对象
在本地治里 SVMCH & RC 进行的横断面观察研究(2026 年 1 月至 3 月)。四十名经血清学证实患有登革热的成年患者被纳入研究。
核心方法
入院时的 aPTT 是主要暴露变量;观察患者的出血情况和 重症登革热(CDC 2015 标准)。
主要结果
40 名患者中,20 名患者的 aPTT 延长(平均 54.8 ± 9.3 秒),20 名患者的 aPTT 正常(平均 34.6 ± 2.9 秒)。任何出血(RR=3.25;p=0.026)、大量出血(RR=3.00;p=0.048)和重症登革热(RR=3.75;p=0.001)均与aPTT延长密切相关。血小板计数和出血症状之间没有明显的关系。
解释与创新
aPTT 是一种可靠的排除登革热大出血和严重程度的筛查工具,NPV 为 95%。仅血小板计数不足以预测出血风险。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,aPTT 可用作筛查工具,以识别有大出血和 重症登革热 风险的患者,从而有可能改善临床决策。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于单个中心和小样本量,但由于报告的明确的统计关联,证据是强有力的。

Activated Partial Thromboplastin Time (aPTT) as a Predictor of Bleeding Manifestations and Severity in Adult Patients with Dengue – A Cross Sectional Study

Authors: B. ThrilokRam, D. Subrahmanyam
Original abstract (English)
Background: Dengue fever is a leading cause of acute febrile illness in tropical regions. Thrombocytopenia alone correlates poorly with bleeding in dengue. Activated partial thromboplastin time (aPTT) reflects intrinsic coagulation dysfunction and may serve as a superior predictor. Objectives: To assess deranged aPTT as a predictor of bleeding manifestations and to evaluate its association with severity of dengue in adult patients. Methods: Cross-sectional observational study at SVMCH & RC, Puducherry (January–March 2026). Forty adult patients with serologically confirmed dengue were enrolled. aPTT at admission was the primary exposure variable; patients were observed for bleeding and severe dengue (CDC 2015 criteria). Results: Twenty of the forty patients exhibited prolonged aPTT (mean 54.8 ± 9.3 s) and twenty had normal aPTT (mean 34.6 ± 2.9 s). Any bleeding (RR=3.25; p=0.026), substantial bleeding (RR=3.00; p=0.048), and severe dengue (RR=3.75; p=0.001) were all strongly correlated with prolonged aPTT. For significant bleeding, the negative predictive value (NPV) was 95.0%. There was no discernible relationship between platelet count and bleeding symptoms. Conclusion: aPTT is a reliable rule-out screening tool for major bleeding and severity in dengue, with an NPV of 95%. Platelet count alone is insufficient to predict bleeding risk.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue fever is a leading cause of acute febrile illness in tropical regions. Thrombocytopenia alone correlates poorly with bleeding in dengue.
Design and population
Cross-sectional observational study at SVMCH & RC, Puducherry (January–March 2026). Forty adult patients with serologically confirmed dengue were enrolled.
Core methods
aPTT at admission was the primary exposure variable; patients were observed for bleeding and severe dengue (CDC 2015 criteria).
Main results
Twenty of the forty patients exhibited prolonged aPTT (mean 54.8 ± 9.3 s) and twenty had normal aPTT (mean 34.6 ± 2.9 s). Any bleeding (RR=3.25; p=0.026), substantial bleeding (RR=3.00; p=0.048), and severe dengue (RR=3.75; p=0.001) were all strongly correlated with prolonged aPTT. There was no discernible relationship between platelet count and bleeding symptoms.
Interpretation and novelty
aPTT is a reliable rule-out screening tool for major bleeding and severity in dengue, with an NPV of 95%. Platelet count alone is insufficient to predict bleeding risk.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that aPTT could be used as a screening tool to identify patients at risk of major bleeding and severe dengue, potentially improving clinical decision-making.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a single center and a small sample size, but the evidence is strong due to the clear statistical associations reported.

登革热 Mangkupalas 健康中心社区预防教育

Dengue Fever Prevention Education For Communities At Mangkupalas Health Center
作者： Siti Salmah, Vivia Lolita Cahyasari Putri Windyaningrum, Hikmah Hikmah, Yuliani Winarti
摘要中文翻译登革热仍然是普斯克玛斯曼格帕拉斯运营区域内的重要公共卫生挑战,而社区成员对其预防的基线知识仍显不足。此次活动旨在通过清除蚊虫巢穴,如排水、覆盖、回收使用过的物品以及补充性预防措施,提高公众对登革热控制的认识。教育项目于2026年5月23日举行,通过互动讲座、讨论、问答环节和传单,吸引了20名参与者。知识水平通过在干预前和干预后立即发放的10题问卷进行评估。平均得分从6,40提高到9,45。知识良好的参与者从4人(20%)增加到18人(90%),而知识较差的参与者则从16人(80%)减少到2人(10%)。威尔科克森符号秩检验证实了教育前后得分存在显著差异(Z = -3,871;p = 0,001)。这些结果表明,在教育干预后,知识水平在短期内有所提高,但需要持续监测以评估预防行为在家庭中的实际应用情况。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热仍然是 Puskesmas Mangkupalas 运营区域内的一项重大公共卫生挑战,而社区成员对其预防的基本知识仍然不足。
设计与对象
这项社区服务活动旨在提高公众对媒介控制的认识,通过排水、覆盖、回收旧物品和补充预防措施消灭蚊巢。该教育计划于 2026 年 5 月 23 日进行,通过互动讲座、讨论、问答环节和传单吸引了 20 名参与者。
核心方法
在干预前和干预后立即使用包含 10 项的调查问卷来评估知识水平。
主要结果
平均分数从6,40升至9,45。良好知识类别的参与者从4人(20%)增加到18人(90%),而较差类别的参与者从16人(80%)减少到2人(10%)。
解释与创新
这些发现表明,教育干预后知识的短期改善。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,有针对性的教育干预措施可以提高社区有关登革热预防的知识,这可能有助于更好的 媒介 控制实践并减少疾病 媒介。
局限与证据强度
Wilcoxon 符号秩检验证实教育课程前后的分数存在显着差异(Z = -3,871;p = 0,001)。该研究仅限于短期评估,并未评估干预措施对实际预防行为的长期影响。

Dengue Fever Prevention Education For Communities At Mangkupalas Health Center

Authors: Siti Salmah, Vivia Lolita Cahyasari Putri Windyaningrum, Hikmah Hikmah, Yuliani Winarti
Original abstract (English)
Dengue remains a significant public health challenge within the operational area of Puskesmas Mangkupalas, while the baseline knowledge of community members regarding its prevention remains inadequate. This community service activity aims to enhance public awareness concerning vector control through mosquito nest eradication via draining, covering, recycling used items, and supplementary preventive measures. The educational program was conducted on May 23, 2026, engaging 20 participants through interactive lectures, discussions, question-and-answer sessions, and leaflets. Knowledge levels were assessed using a 10-item questionnaire administered before and immediately after the intervention. The average score increased from 6,40 to 9,45. Participants classified under the good knowledge category rose from 4 individuals (20%) to 18 individuals (90%), whereas those in the poor category decreased from 16 individuals (80%) to 2 individuals (10%). The Wilcoxon signed-rank test confirmed a significant difference in scores before and after the education session (Z = -3,871; p = 0,001). These findings indicate a short-term improvement in knowledge following the educational intervention, while continued monitoring remains necessary to evaluate the practical application of preventive behaviors within households
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue remains a significant public health challenge within the operational area of Puskesmas Mangkupalas, while the baseline knowledge of community members regarding its prevention remains inadequate.
Design and population
This community service activity aims to enhance public awareness concerning vector control through mosquito nest eradication via draining, covering, recycling used items, and supplementary preventive measures. The educational program was conducted on May 23, 2026, engaging 20 participants through interactive lectures, discussions, question-and-answer sessions, and leaflets.
Core methods
Knowledge levels were assessed using a 10-item questionnaire administered before and immediately after the intervention.
Main results

Interpretation and novelty
These findings indicate a short-term improvement in knowledge following the educational intervention.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that targeted educational interventions can improve community knowledge about dengue prevention, which may contribute to better vector control practices and reduce disease transmission.
Limitations and evidence strength
The Wilcoxon signed-rank test confirmed a significant difference in scores before and after the education session (Z = -3,871; p = 0,001). The study is limited to a short-term assessment and does not evaluate the long-term impact of the intervention on actual preventive behaviors.

为前往流行地区的国际学生提供有关疟疾和登革热的旅行健康准备手册

Bukti Terkini Pencegahan dan Penatalaksanaan Dengue pada Pasien Dewasa

Authors: I Gusti Sri Agung Jaya Kusumadewi
Original abstract (English)
Infeksi virus dengue (DENV) merupakan arbovirosis dengan laju penyebaran tercepat di dunia dan beban yang terus meningkat di Indonesia. Tahun 2024 tercatat sebagai tahun dengan jumlah kasus tertinggi yang pernah dilaporkan kepada Badan Kesehatan Dunia (WHO), dan Indonesia menempati posisi kedua terbesar di dunia. Dalam dua tahun terakhir, lanskap tata laksana dan pencegahan dengue berubah secara substansial: WHO menerbitkan pedoman klinis berbasis GRADE untuk penyakit arboviral pada Juli 2025, vaksin tetravalen TAK-003 memperoleh rekomendasi WHO dan data efektivitas dunia nyata pertamanya, vaksin dosis tunggal Butantan-DV melaporkan hasil fase 3, sementara kandidat antivirus spesifik pertama memasuki dan sebagian dihentikan pada uji klinis fase 2. Tinjauan pustaka ini merangkum bukti terkini mengenai epidemiologi, patogenesis, diagnosis, pencegahan, dan penatalaksanaan infeksi dengue dengan penekanan pada pasien dewasa serta konteks endemis Indonesia. Diagnosis awal berfokus pada pemilihan pemeriksaan yang sesuai dengan hari sakit; meta-analisis terbaru menunjukkan sensitivitas enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay (ELISA) antigen nonstruktural 1 (NS1) sebanding dengan reverse transcription polymerase chain reaction (RT-PCR) pada fase awal. Pencegahan bergeser dari pengendalian vektor berbasis insektisida, yang efektivitasnya menurun karena terjadinya resistensi, menuju pendekatan biologis Wolbachia dan vaksinasi. Penatalaksanaan tetap bersifat suportif dengan terapi utama yaitu resusitasi cairan kristaloid; WHO 2025 secara eksplisit menyarankan tidak menggunakan kortikosteroid sistemik maupun transfusi trombosit profilaksis, dan merekomendasikan kadar laktat serta waktu pengisian kapiler sebagai pemandu terapi cairan. Keterbatasan saat ini adalah ketiadaan terapi antivirus spesifik, keterbatasan data efikasi vaksin terhadap serotipe DENV-3 dan DENV-4 pada individu seronegatif, serta minimnya uji acak terkendali pada populasi dewasa.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Infeksi virus dengue (DENV) merupakan arbovirosis dengan laju penyebaran tercepat di dunia dan beban yang terus meningkat di Indonesia. Tahun 2024 tercatat sebagai tahun dengan jumlah kasus tertinggi yang pernah dilaporkan kepada Badan Kesehatan Dunia (WHO), dan Indonesia menempati posisi kedua terbesar di dunia.

Diagnosis awal berfokus pada pemilihan pemeriksaan yang sesuai dengan hari sakit; meta-analisis terbaru menunjukkan sensitivitas enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay (ELISA) antigen nonstruktural 1 (NS1) sebanding dengan reverse transcription polymerase chain reaction (RT-PCR) pada fase awal.
Main results
Tinjauan pustaka ini merangkum bukti terkini mengenai epidemiologi, patogenesis, diagnosis, pencegahan, dan penatalaksanaan infeksi dengue dengan penekanan pada pasien dewasa serta konteks endemis Indonesia.
Interpretation and novelty
Pencegahan bergeser dari pengendalian vektor berbasis insektisida, yang efektivitasnya menurun karena terjadinya resistensi, menuju pendekatan biologis Wolbachia dan vaksinasi.
Scientific and public-health significance
Penatalaksanaan tetap bersifat suportif dengan terapi utama yaitu resusitasi cairan kristaloid; WHO 2025 secara eksplisit menyarankan tidak menggunakan kortikosteroid sistemik maupun transfusi trombosit profilaksis, dan merekomendasikan kadar laktat serta waktu pengisian kapiler sebagai pemandu terapi cairan.
Limitations and evidence strength
Keterbatasan saat ini adalah ketiadaan terapi antivirus spesifik, keterbatasan data efikasi vaksin terhadap serotipe DENV-3 dan DENV-4 pada individu seronegatif, serta minimnya uji acak terkendali pada populasi dewasa.

单侧登革热相关神经视网膜炎:病例报告

Unilateral dengue-associated neuroretinitis: a case report
作者： Libor Eichenmann, Pavel Kozner, David Jilich, Pavel Pochop, Pavel Kožner
摘要中文翻译摘要 我们报告了一名 33 岁男性,在前往泰国旅行期间患上发热性疾病后,出现右眼​​急性视力丧失的罕见病例,与登革热感染相关的单侧神经视网膜炎。Although ocular manifestations of dengue are well recognized,they are typically bilateral,making unilateral involvement uncommon. Ophthalmic examination revealed unilateral optic disc oedema,retinal thickening involving the macula,vitritis,and retinal haemorrhages.光学相干断层扫描显示明显的浆液性视网膜脱离,并伴有明显的中央增厚,而荧光素血管造影证实视神经盘和视网膜血管渗漏与受影响眼睛的神经视网膜炎和视网膜血管炎一致。Extensive neurological and infectious work-up excluded alternative causes of neuroretinitis. Serological testing confirmed a pattern consistent with recent dengue infection.在排除感染活动期后,患者接受全身皮质类固醇治疗,导致解剖结构迅速改善,视力显着恢复,并在长期随访中保持稳定。该病例强调登革热相关神经视网膜炎可能单侧出现,即使在非典型表现中,在回国旅行者后段炎症的鉴别诊断中也应考虑登革热感染。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在报告一例与登革热感染相关的罕见单侧神经视网膜炎病例,强调需要将登革热视为回国旅行者非典型眼部炎症的潜在原因。
设计与对象
病例报告 是对一名 33 岁男子进行的,他在泰国旅行期间患上发热性疾病,导致右眼急性视力丧失。
核心方法
患者接受了眼科检查、光学相干断层扫描和荧光素血管造影以评估视网膜变化。进行了血清学检测以确认登革热感染,并进行了广泛的神经学和感染性检查以排除其他原因。
主要结果
眼科检查发现单侧视盘水肿、累及黄斑的视网膜增厚、玻璃体炎和视网膜出血。光学相干断层扫描显示明显的浆液性视网膜脱离,并伴有明显的中央增厚,而荧光素血管造影证实视神经盘和视网膜血管渗漏与受影响眼睛的神经视网膜炎和视网膜血管炎一致。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,登革热相关的神经视网膜炎可以单侧出现,这并不常见,并强调了在回国旅行者后段炎症鉴别 诊断 中考虑登革热感染的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
该病例强调在诊断登革热相关眼部并发症时需要保持警惕,特别是从流行地区返回的旅行者,并强调可能出现非典型表现,可能需要特定的诊断方法。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于单个 病例报告,可能无法推广。然而,证据是强有力的,因为它包括详细的临床和诊断结果,并且患者的病情是通过血清学检测和影像学证实的。

Unilateral dengue-associated neuroretinitis: a case report

Authors: Libor Eichenmann, Pavel Kozner, David Jilich, Pavel Pochop, Pavel Kožner
Original abstract (English)
Abstract We report a rare case of unilateral neuroretinitis associated with dengue infection in a 33-year-old man presenting with acute visual loss in the right eye following a febrile illness acquired during travel to Thailand. Although ocular manifestations of dengue are well recognized, they are typically bilateral, making unilateral involvement uncommon. Ophthalmic examination revealed unilateral optic disc oedema, retinal thickening involving the macula, vitritis, and retinal haemorrhages. Optical coherence tomography demonstrated marked serous retinal detachment with significant central thickening, while fluorescein angiography confirmed optic disc and retinal vascular leakage consistent with neuroretinitis and retinal vasculitis in the affected eye. Extensive neurological and infectious work-up excluded alternative causes of neuroretinitis. Serological testing confirmed a pattern consistent with recent dengue infection. The patient was treated with systemic corticosteroids following exclusion of active phase of infection, resulting in rapid anatomical improvement and significant visual recovery, which remained stable at long-term follow-up. This case highlights that dengue-associated neuroretinitis may present unilaterally, and dengue infection should be considered in the differential diagnosis of posterior segment inflammation in returning travellers, even in atypical presentations.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to report a rare case of unilateral neuroretinitis associated with dengue infection, highlighting the need to consider dengue as a potential cause of atypical ocular inflammation in returning travelers.
Design and population
A case report was conducted on a 33-year-old man who developed acute visual loss in the right eye following a febrile illness acquired during travel to Thailand.
Core methods
The patient underwent ophthalmic examination, optical coherence tomography, and fluorescein angiography to assess retinal changes. Serological testing was performed to confirm dengue infection, and extensive neurological and infectious work-up was conducted to exclude alternative causes.
Main results
Ophthalmic examination revealed unilateral optic disc oedema, retinal thickening involving the macula, vitritis, and retinal haemorrhages. Optical coherence tomography demonstrated marked serous retinal detachment with significant central thickening, while fluorescein angiography confirmed optic disc and retinal vascular leakage consistent with neuroretinitis and retinal vasculitis in the affected eye.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that dengue-associated neuroretinitis can present unilaterally, which is uncommon, and emphasize the importance of considering dengue infection in the differential diagnosis of posterior segment inflammation in returning travelers.
Scientific and public-health significance
This case underscores the need for vigilance in diagnosing dengue-related ocular complications, particularly in travelers returning from endemic regions, and highlights the potential for atypical presentations that may require specific diagnostic approaches.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a single case report, which may not be generalizable. However, the evidence is strong as it includes detailed clinical and diagnostic findings, and the patient's condition was confirmed through serological testing and imaging.

2020 年至 2025 年阿拉戈斯登革热事件与社会经济脆弱性之间的关系 登革热 发病率 与阿拉戈斯各市社会经济脆弱性之间的关系2020 年和 2025 年 2020 年和 2025 年阿拉戈斯市的登革热事件和社会经济脆弱性关系

RELAÇÃO ENTRE A INCIDÊNCIA DE DENGUE E VULNERABILIDADE SOCIOECONÔMICA NOS MUNICÍPIOS DE ALAGOAS ENTRE 2020 E 2025 RELATIONSHIP BETWEEN DENGUE INCIDENCE AND SOCIOECONOMIC VULNERABILITY IN THE MUNICIPALITIES OF ALAGOAS BETWEEN 2020 AND 2025 RELACIÓN ENTRE LA INCIDENCIA DEL DENGUE Y LA VULNERABILIDAD SOCIOECONÓMICA EN LOS MUNICIPIOS DE ALAGOAS ENTRE 2020 Y 2025
作者： Camylle Victória Marques de Araújo Farias, Lucilo José Ribeiro Neto, Laércio Pol Fachin
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查 2020 年至 2025 年间登革热 媒介 与阿拉戈斯市社会经济脆弱性之间的关系。登革热是一个重大的公共卫生问题,特别是在巴西等热带和亚热带国家,那里的社会不平等和环境条件可能会影响 媒介 的暴露和获得医疗保健的机会。
设计与对象
该研究是通过 TabNet 平台使用 SIAN/DATASUS 的二手数据进行的流行病学分析,考虑了可能病例、通知城市和首次出现症状的年份。其中包括 20 个城市,其中 10 个城市的社会脆弱性指数 (IVS) 较高,10 个城市的社会脆弱性指数较低。
核心方法
该研究采用了通过 TabNet 获得的 SINAN/DATASUS 二手数据,并使用了 IPEA 提供的社会脆弱性指数 (IVS)。它分析了可能的登革热病例、其通知城市以及首次出现症状的年份,以评估社会经济脆弱性与登革热 发病率 之间的关系。
主要结果
The study observed higher average incidence rates in municipalities with lower IVS across all years, with a notable difference in 2022, where the rates were 1,269.99 and 338.76 cases per 100,000 inhabitants in the lower and higher IVS groups, respectively. The Spearman correlation was negative in all years, ranging from ρ = -0.204 to ρ = -0.526, with the strongest correlation in 2022 (ρ = -0.526; p<0.05).
解释与创新
研究结果表明,仅靠社会经济脆弱性并不能完全解释登革热病例的分布,突出表明需要进一步研究影响登革热 传播 因素的复杂相互作用。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以通过强调解决登革热预防和控制工作中社会经济差异的重要性,为公共卫生战略提供信息,特别是在脆弱程度不同的地区。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对二手数据的依赖以及可能存在未测量的混杂因素而受到限制。证据是中等的,因为分析是基于观察数据而没有直接干预或实验验证。

RELAÇÃO ENTRE A INCIDÊNCIA DE DENGUE E VULNERABILIDADE SOCIOECONÔMICA NOS MUNICÍPIOS DE ALAGOAS ENTRE 2020 E 2025 RELATIONSHIP BETWEEN DENGUE INCIDENCE AND SOCIOECONOMIC VULNERABILITY IN THE MUNICIPALITIES OF ALAGOAS BETWEEN 2020 AND 2025 RELACIÓN ENTRE LA INCIDENCIA DEL DENGUE Y LA VULNERABILIDAD SOCIOECONÓMICA EN LOS MUNICIPIOS DE ALAGOAS ENTRE 2020 Y 2025

Authors: Camylle Victória Marques de Araújo Farias, Lucilo José Ribeiro Neto, Laércio Pol Fachin
Original abstract (English)
A dengue é uma doença de grande relevância para a saúde pública, especialmente em países tropicais e subtropicais como o Brasil. Sua ocorrência pode estar relacionada a desigualdades sociais e condições ambientais que influenciam a exposição ao vetor e o acesso aos serviços de saúde. Este estudo analisou a relação entre a incidência de dengue e vulnerabilidade social nos municípios de Alagoas, entre 2020 e 2025. Foi realizado estudo epidemiológico com dados secundários do SINAN/DATASUS, obtidos pela plataforma TabNet, considerando casos prováveis, município de notificação e ano do primeiro sintoma. Também foram utilizados os Índices de Vulnerabilidade Social (IVS) disponibilizados pelo IPEA. Foram analisados 20 municípios, sendo 10 com maior e 10 com menor IVS. Observou-se maior incidência média entre os municípios de menor IVS em todos os anos, com destaque para 2022, quando as médias foram de 1.269,99 e 338,76 casos/100 mil habitantes nos grupos de menor e maior IVS, respectivamente. A correlação de Spearman foi negativa em todos os anos, variando de ρ = -0,204 a ρ = -0,526, com maior magnitude em 2022 (ρ = -0,526; p<0,05). Os resultados sugerem que a vulnerabilidade social, isoladamente, não explica a distribuição da dengue.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the relationship between dengue incidence and socioeconomic vulnerability in the municipalities of Alagoas between 2020 and 2025. Dengue is a significant public health issue, particularly in tropical and subtropical countries like Brazil, where social inequalities and environmental conditions may influence vector exposure and access to healthcare.
Design and population
The study was an epidemiological analysis using secondary data from SINAN/DATASUS through the TabNet platform, considering probable cases, notification municipality, and the year of first symptoms. It included 20 municipalities, with 10 having higher and 10 having lower Social Vulnerability Indices (IVS).
Core methods
The study employed secondary data from SINAN/DATASUS, obtained via TabNet, and used Social Vulnerability Indices (IVS) provided by IPEA. It analyzed probable dengue cases, their notification municipality, and the year of first symptoms to assess the relationship between socioeconomic vulnerability and dengue incidence.
Main results
The study observed higher average incidence rates in municipalities with lower IVS across all years, with a notable difference in 2022, where the rates were 1,269.99 and 338.76 cases per 100,000 inhabitants in the lower and higher IVS groups, respectively. The Spearman correlation was negative in all years, ranging from ρ = -0.204 to ρ = -0.526, with the strongest correlation in 2022 (ρ = -0.526; p<0.05).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that socioeconomic vulnerability alone does not fully explain the distribution of dengue cases, highlighting the need for further research into the complex interplay of factors affecting dengue transmission.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results can inform public health strategies by emphasizing the importance of addressing socioeconomic disparities in dengue prevention and control efforts, particularly in regions with varying levels of vulnerability.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on secondary data and the potential for unmeasured confounding factors. The evidence is moderate, as the analysis is based on observational data without direct intervention or experimental validation.

登革热 发病率 与无幼虫指数之间的空间关联:印度尼西亚泗水的双变量空间自相关分析

Spatial Association Between Dengue Incidence and Larvae-Free Index: A Bivariate Spatial Autocorrelation Analysis in Surabaya, Indonesia
作者： Khuliyah Candraning Diyanah, Ririh Yudhastuti, Lilis Sulistyorini, Hari Basuki Notobroto, Zida Husnina, Muhammad Rasyid Ridha, Babucarr Jassey, Aditya Sukma Pawitra, Diva Alishya Shafwah, Rahmat Dapari
摘要中文翻译简介:印度尼西亚泗水的登革热 传播 因城中村而异,但其与 媒介 控制绩效的空间关系仍不清楚。本研究检查了登革热 发病率、无幼虫指数 (LFI) 及其双变量空间关联。方法:这项空间生态研究分析了从泗水市卫生办公室获得的 2020-2025 年泗水所有 154 个城中村的年度确诊登革热病例和 LFI 数据。发病率 按每 10,000 人计算。乘法季节性分解表征了每月病例模式。全球 Moran’s I 和局部空间关联指标 (LISA) 评估空间聚类,而双变量 Moran’s I 和双变量 LISA (BiLISA) 检查局部登革热 发病率 与邻近 LFI 值之间的关联。结果和讨论:登革热 发病率 在六年中的五年内显着聚集(I = 0.0784–0.2727),2024 年除外(I = 0.0280;p = 0.222)。LFI 每年都会显着聚集(I = 0.2190–0.3087;p = 0.001)。双变量 Moran’s I 在五年内为负值,并在 2020 年(−0.0805;p = 0.022)、2021 年(−0.0875;p = 0.019)和 2025 年(−0.1046;p = 0.003)显着,表明较高的 发病率 和较低的邻近 LFI 之间存在空间关联。2025 年,LISA 确定了 10 个高-高和 15 个低-低登革热集群。BiLISA 每年都会确定高低村庄,其中 Ujung 和 Pegirian 出现频率最高。结论:登革热发病率和LFI表现出空间聚集性,三年内具有显着的空间逆相关性。在确定目标 媒介 控制的优先领域时,LFI 应补充而不是取代登革热 监测。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在研究印度尼西亚泗水登革热 发病率 与无幼虫指数 (LFI) 之间的空间关系,因为登革热 传播 因城中村而异,且与 媒介 控制绩效的空间关联仍不清楚。
设计与对象
2020年至2025年间,利用每年确诊的登革热病例和从泗水市卫生办公室获得的LFI数据,对印度尼西亚泗水的所有154个城中村进行了空间生态研究。
核心方法
登革热 发病率 按每 10,000 人计算,并使用乘法季节性分解来表征每月病例模式。使用全局 Moran’s I 和局部空间关联指标 (LISA) 评估空间聚类,而双变量 Moran’s I 和双变量 LISA (BiLISA) 检查局部登革热 发病率 与邻近 LFI 值之间的关联。
主要结果
Global Moran's I 表明登革热 发病率 在六年中的五年内出现显着聚集(I = 0.0784–0.2727),2024 年除外(I = 0.0280;p = 0.222)。LFI 每年都会显着聚集(I = 0.2190–0.3087;p = 0.001)。双变量 Moran’s I 在五年内为负值,在 2020 年、2021 年和 2025 年显着,显示较高的登革热 发病率 和较低的邻近 LFI 之间存在逆空间关联。
解释与创新
该研究发现三年内登革热发病率与LFI之间存在显着的逆空间关联,这表明LFI值较低的区域可能与登革热发病率较高相关,这是理解登革热传播空间动态的一个新发现。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以通过突出显示较低 LFI 值与较高登革热 监测 相关的区域,为有针对性的 媒介 控制策略提供信息,从而有助于确定 监测 和干预工作的优先顺序。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于空间生态分析,并未考虑影响登革热传播的其他因素。证据是中等的,因为它依赖于汇总数据和空间统计方法,而没有个体层面的分析。

Spatial Association Between Dengue Incidence and Larvae-Free Index: A Bivariate Spatial Autocorrelation Analysis in Surabaya, Indonesia

Authors: Khuliyah Candraning Diyanah, Ririh Yudhastuti, Lilis Sulistyorini, Hari Basuki Notobroto, Zida Husnina, Muhammad Rasyid Ridha, Babucarr Jassey, Aditya Sukma Pawitra, Diva Alishya Shafwah, Rahmat Dapari
Original abstract (English)
Introduction: Dengue transmission in Surabaya, Indonesia, varies across urban villages, but its spatial relationship with vector-control performance remains insufficiently understood. This study examined dengue incidence, the Larvae-Free Index (LFI), and their bivariate spatial association. Methods: This spatial ecological study analysed annual confirmed dengue cases and LFI data from all 154 urban villages in Surabaya during 2020–2025, obtained from the Surabaya City Health Office. Incidence was calculated per 10,000 population. Multiplicative seasonal decomposition characterised monthly case patterns. Global Moran’s I and Local Indicators of Spatial Association (LISA) assessed spatial clustering, while Bivariate Moran’s I and Bivariate LISA (BiLISA) examined associations between local dengue incidence and neighbouring LFI values. Results and Discussion: Dengue incidence clustered significantly in five of six years (I = 0.0784–0.2727), except 2024 (I = 0.0280; p = 0.222). LFI clustered significantly every year (I = 0.2190–0.3087; p = 0.001). Bivariate Moran’s I was negative in five years and significant in 2020 (−0.0805; p = 0.022), 2021 (−0.0875; p = 0.019), and 2025 (−0.1046; p = 0.003), indicating spatial associations between higher incidence and lower neighbouring LFI. In 2025, LISA identified 10 High–High and 15 Low–Low dengue clusters. BiLISA identified High–Low villages annually, with Ujung and Pegirian recurring most frequently. Conclusion: Dengue incidence and LFI exhibited spatial clustering, with significant inverse spatial associations in three years. LFI should complement, rather than replace, dengue surveillance when identifying priority areas for targeted vector control.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to examine the spatial relationship between dengue incidence and the Larvae-Free Index (LFI) in Surabaya, Indonesia, as dengue transmission varies across urban villages and the spatial association with vector-control performance remains insufficiently understood.
Design and population
A spatial ecological study was conducted in all 154 urban villages of Surabaya, Indonesia, during the years 2020–2025, using annual confirmed dengue cases and LFI data obtained from the Surabaya City Health Office.
Core methods
Dengue incidence was calculated per 10,000 population, and monthly case patterns were characterised using multiplicative seasonal decomposition. Spatial clustering was assessed using Global Moran’s I and Local Indicators of Spatial Association (LISA), while Bivariate Moran’s I and Bivariate LISA (BiLISA) examined associations between local dengue incidence and neighbouring LFI values.
Main results
Global Moran’s I indicated significant dengue incidence clustering in five of six years (I = 0.0784–0.2727), except 2024 (I = 0.0280; p = 0.222). LFI clustered significantly every year (I = 0.2190–0.3087; p = 0.001). Bivariate Moran’s I was negative in five years and significant in 2020, 2021, and 2025, showing inverse spatial associations between higher dengue incidence and lower neighbouring LFI.
Interpretation and novelty
The study found significant inverse spatial associations between dengue incidence and LFI in three years, suggesting that areas with lower LFI values may be associated with higher dengue incidence, which is a novel finding in understanding spatial dynamics of dengue transmission.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings can inform targeted vector control strategies by highlighting areas where lower LFI values correlate with higher dengue incidence, thus aiding in the prioritisation of surveillance and intervention efforts.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to spatial ecological analysis and does not account for other factors influencing dengue transmission. The evidence is moderate as it relies on aggregated data and spatial statistical methods without individual-level analysis.

2025 年巴葛阿南市 Pengaringan 社区卫生中心环境卫生与登革出血热 (DHF) 发病率 的关系

The Relationship Between Environmental Sanitation and the Incidence of Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever (DHF) at Pengaringan Community Health Center, Pagar Alam City, in 2025
作者： Batria Sulastri, Tri Komalasari, Nesa Wahyuni, Darika Reforma, Ovisen Imresah
摘要中文翻译登革热 的 发病率 在全球范围内急剧增加。登革热的发病率是环境卫生因素造成的。根据Pagaralam市卫生办公室的数据,截至2025年2月,2025年登革热病例将增加至38例,这一显着增加使研究人员希望从环境卫生方面分析登革热的原因。本研究的目的是确定帕加拉拉姆市排水卫生中心 2025 年环境卫生与登革出血热 (DHF) 发病率 之间的关系。研究方法采用横断面法,对 162 名受访者进行了意外抽样。结果显示,环境卫生与登革热发病率相关的变量为房屋环境条件p值0.004、水储存宿主 p值0.013和牲畜笼舍卫生p值0.000。SPAL和废物处理系统与登革热发病率没有关系。由此可见,环境卫生与登革热发病率相关,其中最主要的因素是牲畜笼舍的卫生。建议帕加拉拉姆市卫生办公室能够创建一个与“每个孩子都是 Jumantik”相关的计划,该计划可以由卫生办公室与学校和周边社区合作开展。学校可以指派每个孩子在各自的家里担任陪审员。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查2025年Pagar Alam市Pengaringan社区卫生中心的环境卫生与登革出血热(DHF)发病率之间的关系。背景强调了全球登革热病例的增加以及环境因素在其传播中的潜在作用。
设计与对象
2025 年,Pagar Alam 市 Pengaringan 社区健康中心进行了一项横断面研究,涉及通过意外抽样选出的 162 名受访者。该研究的重点是分析登革热病例的 发病率 与环境卫生因素的关系。
核心方法
该研究采用横断面设计和意外抽样来收集 162 名受访者的数据。评估了环境卫生因素,包括房屋环境条件、水储存宿主和牲畜笼卫生,以确定它们与登革热发病率的关联。
主要结果
结果表明,房屋环境条件(p = 0.004)、水储存宿主(p = 0.013)和牲畜笼子卫生(p = 0.000)与登革热发病率显着相关。然而,没有发现SPAL与登革热发病率的废物处理系统之间存在任何关系。
解释与创新
作者对研究结果的解释表明,环境卫生因素,特别是牲畜笼子卫生,在登革热的 发病率 中发挥着关键作用。这项研究有助于了解当地卫生条件对登革热 传播 的影响。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以通过强调改善环境卫生以减少登革热 发病率 的重要性,为当地公共卫生战略提供信息。具体而言,重点关注牲畜笼舍卫生可能是一个关键干预领域。
局限与证据强度
该研究的横截面设计限制了其建立因果关系的能力,并且对偶然抽样的依赖可能会引入偏差。尽管存在这些局限性,但由于研究结果的统计显着性,证据的强度适中。

The Relationship Between Environmental Sanitation and the Incidence of Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever (DHF) at Pengaringan Community Health Center, Pagar Alam City, in 2025

Authors: Batria Sulastri, Tri Komalasari, Nesa Wahyuni, Darika Reforma, Ovisen Imresah
Original abstract (English)
The incidence of dengue fever has increased dramatically worldwide. The incidence of dengue fever is caused by environmental sanitation factors. In 2025 there will be an increase in dengue cases to 38 cases as of February 2025 based on data from the Pagaralam City Health Office, this significant increase makes researchers want to analyze the causes of dengue in terms of environmental sanitation. The purpose of this study is to determine the relationship between environmental sanitation and the incidence of dengue hemorrhagic fever (DHF) at the Pagaralam City Drainage Health Center in 2025. The research method used Cross Sectional with a sample of 162 respondents taken by accidental sampling. The results showed that the variables related to the incidence of dengue from environmental sanitation were the environmental condition of the house p value 0.004, water reservoir p value 0.013, and livestock cage sanitation p value 0.000. There was no relationship between SPAL and the waste disposal system with the incidence of dengue. It can be concluded that environmental sanitation is related to the incidence of dengue with the most dominant factor being the sanitation of livestock cages. It is recommended for the Pagaralam City Health Office to be able to create a program related to "Every Child is Jumantik", this program can be carried out by the Health Office in collaboration with schools and the surrounding community. Schools can assign each child to be a jumantik in their respective homes.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the relationship between environmental sanitation and the incidence of dengue hemorrhagic fever (DHF) in Pengaringan Community Health Center, Pagar Alam City, in 2025. The background highlights the global increase in dengue fever cases and the potential role of environmental factors in its spread.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted in 2025 at Pengaringan Community Health Center, Pagar Alam City, involving 162 respondents selected through accidental sampling. The study focused on analyzing the incidence of dengue cases in relation to environmental sanitation factors.
Core methods
The study employed a cross-sectional design with accidental sampling to collect data from 162 respondents. Environmental sanitation factors were assessed, including house environmental conditions, water reservoirs, and livestock cage sanitation, to determine their association with dengue incidence.
Main results
The results indicated that house environmental conditions (p = 0.004), water reservoirs (p = 0.013), and livestock cage sanitation (p = 0.000) were significantly related to the incidence of dengue. However, there was no relationship found between SPAL and the waste disposal system with dengue incidence.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings to suggest that environmental sanitation factors, particularly livestock cage sanitation, play a critical role in the incidence of dengue. This study contributes to understanding the local impact of sanitation on dengue transmission.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform local public health strategies by highlighting the importance of improving environmental sanitation to reduce dengue incidence. Specifically, focusing on livestock cage sanitation may be a key intervention area.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's cross-sectional design limits its ability to establish causality, and the reliance on accidental sampling may introduce bias. Despite these limitations, the evidence is moderate in strength due to the statistical significance of the findings.

登革热感染中非结构蛋白 1 抗原血症与白细胞减少症之间的关联

Association Between Nonstructural Protein 1 Antigenemia and Leukopenia in Dengue Infection
作者： G. Praveen, Anusha Gopinathan, S. S. Jayalakshmi, Leela Kakithakara Vajravelu, Kalaivani P, Kakithakara Vajravelu Leela, Kalaivani Amitkumar
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热是热带和亚热带地区广泛传播的蚊媒疾病。白细胞减少症是登革热感染中常见的血液学异常;然而,登革热血清学标志物与白细胞(WBC)计数之间的关联尚未得到很好的表征。
设计与对象
对 2025 年 10 月至 12 月期间在三级甲等教学医院就诊的 457 名血清学确诊的登革热患者进行了一项回顾性观察研究。其中 288 名患者 (63.0%) NS1 抗原呈阳性,264 名患者 (57.8%) 登革热 IgM 抗体呈阳性。
核心方法
人口统计特征、登革热 NS1 抗原状态、登革热 IgM 抗体状态和白细胞计数均从实验室记录中获得。白细胞计数分为白细胞减少(< 11,000 个细胞/mm 3 )和白细胞增多(> 11,000 个细胞/mm 3 )。使用皮尔逊卡方检验分析血清学标志物与白细胞计数类别之间的关联。在适当的情况下计算了 95% CI 的优势比 (OR)。
主要结果
457 例患者中,145 例(31.7%)白细胞减少,305 例(66.7%)计数正常,7 例(1.5%)白细胞增多。288 名 NS1 抗原阳性患者中有 128 名出现白细胞减少症 (44.4%),而 169 名 NS1 抗原阴性患者中有 17 名出现白细胞减少症 (10.1%)(Fisher 精确检验,P < 0.001;OR:7.15;95% CI:4.12-12.43)。登革热 IgM 抗体阳性显示相反的关系:264 名 IgM 阳性患者中有 63 名 (23.9%) 出现白细胞减少,而 177 名 IgM 阴性患者中有 69 名 (39.0%) 出现白细胞减少(Fisher 精确检验,P < 0.001;OR:0.49;95% CI:0.32-0.74)。
解释与创新
白细胞减少症在 NS1 抗原阳性患者中发生率明显更高,而在 IgM 抗体阳性患者中发生率明显较低,这表明白细胞减少症聚集在登革热感染的早期病毒血症阶段,并随着抗体反应的发展而消退。
科研与公卫意义
白细胞减少症作为 NS1 抗原阳性阶段的标志物具有特异性但不敏感,因此其主要实用价值可能在于告知血清学检测的选择和时机,而不是确认通过 NS1 抗原检测已建立的 诊断。
局限与证据强度
有必要进行前瞻性多中心研究,记录发病当天、连续血液学测量、定量 NS1 抗原水平和临床结果,以验证这些发现。提供的证据没有报告与研究设计或数据收集相关的任何限制。

Association Between Nonstructural Protein 1 Antigenemia and Leukopenia in Dengue Infection

Authors: G. Praveen, Anusha Gopinathan, S. S. Jayalakshmi, Leela Kakithakara Vajravelu, Kalaivani P, Kakithakara Vajravelu Leela, Kalaivani Amitkumar
Original abstract (English)
Background: Dengue is a widespread mosquito-borne illness across tropical and subtropical areas. Leukopenia is a common hematological abnormality in dengue infection; however, the association between dengue serological markers and white blood cell (WBC) count has not been well characterized. Objective: To evaluate the association between dengue nonstructural protein 1 (NS1) antigen positivity, dengue immunoglobulin M (IgM) antibody positivity, and WBC count categories among serologically confirmed dengue patients. Materials and Methods: A retrospective observational study was conducted among 457 serologically confirmed dengue patients attending a tertiary care teaching hospital between October and December 2025. Demographic characteristics, dengue NS1 antigen status, dengue IgM antibody status, and WBC counts were obtained from laboratory records. WBC counts were categorized as leukopenia ( 11,000 cells/mm 3 ). Associations between serological markers and WBC count categories were analyzed using Pearson’s chi-square test. Odds ratios (ORs) with 95% CIs were calculated where appropriate. Results: Among the 457 patients, leukopenia was observed in 145 (31.7%), while 305 (66.7%) had normal counts and 7 (1.5%) had leukocytosis. NS1 antigen was positive in 288 patients (63.0%) and dengue IgM antibody in 264 (57.8%). Leukopenia was present in 128 of 288 NS1 antigen-positive patients (44.4%) compared with 17 of 169 NS1 antigen-negative patients (10.1%) (Fisher exact test, P < 0.001; OR: 7.15; 95% CI: 4.12-12.43). As a marker of NS1 antigen positivity, leukopenia showed a specificity of 89.9% (95% CI: 84.5-93.6) and a positive predictive value of 88.3% (95% CI: 82.0-92.5), but a sensitivity of only 44.4% (95% CI: 38.8-50.2). Dengue IgM antibody positivity showed the opposite relationship: leukopenia was present in 63 of 264 IgM-positive patients (23.9%) compared with 69 of 177 IgM-negative patients (39.0%) (Fisher exact test, P < 0.001; OR: 0.49; 95% CI: 0.32-0.74). Leukopenia was most frequent in the small group with equivocal IgM results (13/16, 81.2%). Conclusions: Leukopenia was markedly more frequent among NS1 antigen-positive patients and markedly less frequent among IgM antibody-positive patients, indicating that leukopenia clusters in the early viremic phase of dengue infection and resolves as the antibody response develops. Leukopenia was specific but insensitive as a marker of the NS1 antigen-positive phase, so its principal practical value may lie in informing the choice and timing of serological testing rather than in confirming a diagnosis already established by NS1 antigen detection. Prospective multicenter studies recording the day of illness at presentation, serial hematological measurements, quantitative NS1 antigen levels, and clinical outcomes are warranted to validate these findings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue is a widespread mosquito-borne illness across tropical and subtropical areas. Leukopenia is a common hematological abnormality in dengue infection; however, the association between dengue serological markers and white blood cell (WBC) count has not been well characterized.

Prospective multicenter studies recording the day of illness at presentation, serial hematological measurements, quantitative NS1 antigen levels, and clinical outcomes are warranted to validate these findings. The supplied evidence does not report any limitations related to the study design or data collection.

基于智能无人机的生物传感器,用于检测基于智能无人机平台的病毒诊断抗原

Intelligent drone-based biosensor for the detection of dengue virus antigen towards smart unmanned aerial-vehicle platform-based virus diagnosis
作者： Mohd. Rahil Hasan, Sameer Khan, Dakshita Naithani, Pradakshina Sharma, Yashomita Mehta, Samra Ansari, Sushil kumar, Ubaid Mushtaq Naikoo, Anupam Baliyan, Mohamed Abbas
摘要中文翻译在当前的工作中,我们建立了一个登革热智能系统诊断,该系统可以进行现场检测,同时无需人为干预和在难以到达的区域进行持续监测。该系统由 4 项智能技术组成:i) 无人机,ii) 生物传感器,iii) Yolov5 人工智能模块,iv) 一个链接患者电子处方条形码的二维码衍生数字网站,其中生物传感器充当智能探测器,无人机充当将生物传感器运输到测试区的车辆。深度学习分析由专家使用人工智能模块完成;系统中还添加了额外的基于二维码的网站,允许用户扫描代码并将检测数据发送给实验室当局,患者还可以从医生那里获得医疗电子处方。拟议的系统采用轻质材料、优化的电源和耐候外壳,以确保可靠的现场诊断。然而,在评估所提出系统的现场适用性和可扩展性时,应考虑与基于无人机的诊断部署相关的实际挑战,包括环境条件、飞行稳定性、电池容量、有效载荷限制和远程设置中的可靠操作。建议的检测平台采用无仪器侧流比色法,能够在 5 秒内肉眼检测 登革病毒 抗原,LOD 为 0.91 μg/mL。因此,基于无人机的方法允许临床医生监测偏远人群中的 登革病毒 抗原。因此,政府和非营利组织可以利用该技术来监控红色警戒区,接收准确可靠的数据以做出明智的决策。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在建立一种登革热智能系统诊断,无需人工干预即可进行现场检测,满足偏远地区可靠诊断的需求。
设计与对象
该系统包括无人机、生物传感器、人工智能模块以及用于数据 传播 和患者电子处方的数字网站,专为在偏远和难以到达的地区使用而设计。
核心方法
使用人工智能模块进行深度学习分析,并添加基于二维码的网站以方便数据传播和患者电子处方。
主要结果
所提出的系统采用了轻质材料、优化的电源和耐候外壳,能够实现可靠的现场诊断,5 秒内检测限低至 0.91 μg/mL。
解释与创新
该系统无需人工干预和连续监控即可执行现场诊断的能力代表了一种 登革病毒 检测的新颖方法。
科研与公卫意义
该系统可供政府和非营利组织用来监测偏远人群的 登革病毒 抗原,为明智的决策提供精确可靠的数据。
局限与证据强度
在评估所提议系统的现场适用性和可扩展性时,应考虑与基于无人机的诊断部署相关的实际挑战,例如环境条件和远程设置中的可靠操作。

Intelligent drone-based biosensor for the detection of dengue virus antigen towards smart unmanned aerial-vehicle platform-based virus diagnosis

Authors: Mohd. Rahil Hasan, Sameer Khan, Dakshita Naithani, Pradakshina Sharma, Yashomita Mehta, Samra Ansari, Sushil kumar, Ubaid Mushtaq Naikoo, Anupam Baliyan, Mohamed Abbas
Original abstract (English)
In the current work, we established a smart system for dengue diagnosis that enables on-site detection while removing the need for human intervention and continuous monitoring in difficult-to-reach areas. The system comprises of 4-smart technologies: i) drone, ii) biosensor, iii) Yolov5 AI-module, iv) a QR-code-derived digital website linked patient E-Prescription barcode, where the biosensor acts as a smart detector, and the drone as a vehicle for transporting the biosensor to the testing zone. Deep learning analysis was done by an expert using an artificial intelligence module; additional QR-code-based websites were added to the system, allowing users to scan the code and send detection data to laboratory authorities, and the patient got a medical e-prescription from the doctor. The proposed system has lightweight materials, optimized power sources, and weather-resistant housing to ensure reliable on-site diagnostics. However, practical challenges associated with drone-based diagnostic deployment, including environmental conditions, flight stability, battery capacity, payload constraints, and reliable operation in remote settings, should be considered when evaluating the field applicability and scalability of the proposed system. The suggested detection platform used the instrument-less lateral flow colorimetric approach, enabling naked-eye detection of dengue virus antigen with an LOD of 0.91 μg/mL within 5 s. As a consequence, the Drone-based approach allows clinicians to monitor the dengue virus antigen in remote populations. Accordingly, governments and non-profit organizations can utilize this technology to monitor the red alert zone, receiving precise and reliable data for informed decision-making.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to establish a smart system for dengue diagnosis that enables on-site detection without human intervention, addressing the need for reliable diagnostics in remote areas.
Design and population
The system includes a drone, biosensor, AI module, and a digital website for data transmission and patient e-prescription, designed for use in remote and difficult-to-reach areas.
Core methods
Deep learning analysis was performed using an AI module, and QR-code-based websites were added to facilitate data transmission and patient e-prescription.
Main results
The proposed system achieved lightweight materials, optimized power sources, and weather-resistant housing, enabling reliable on-site diagnostics with a low detection limit of 0.91 μg/mL within 5 seconds.
Interpretation and novelty
The system's ability to perform on-site diagnostics without human intervention and continuous monitoring represents a novel approach to dengue virus detection.
Scientific and public-health significance
The system can be utilized by governments and non-profit organizations to monitor dengue virus antigen in remote populations, providing precise and reliable data for informed decision-making.
Limitations and evidence strength
Practical challenges associated with drone-based diagnostic deployment, such as environmental conditions and reliable operation in remote settings, should be considered when evaluating the field applicability and scalability of the proposed system.

沃特豪斯-弗里德里希森脑膜炎球菌病和登革热感染综合征:与卡索有关

SÍNDROME DE WATERHOUSE-FRIDERICHSEN POR MENINGOCOCCEMIA E COINFECÇÃO COM DENGUE: RELATO DE CASO
作者： Beatriz Ferreira Cunha, Gabriela Alencar Moura, Sávio Samuel Feitosa Machado, Joel Boechat de Morais Júnior, Joel Boechat de Morais
摘要中文翻译简介:沃特豪斯-弗里德里希森综合症 (SWF) 是一种因肾上腺功能不全、与脑膜炎球菌血症有关、因外因而存在的疾病。登革热和 SWF 的伴随情况非常严重,代表了无法进行全面诊断的情况。目的:与沃特豪斯-弗里德里希森综合症协会有关脑膜炎奈瑟菌和登革热病毒在临床、实验室和解剖学上的感染情况有关。方法:对临床、实验室、宏观和组织病理学进行分析,并通过 Barbalha 检验服务 (CE) 进行尸检。通过与实验室相关的宏观、微观和 SWF 病理学结果相关的实验室检查来诊断感染病原体。结果:男性患者,26 岁,目前发烧,腹部,荨麻疹和脓肿,进化。尸检显示脑膜炎奈瑟菌和 DENV-1 病毒的存在。确认脑膜炎和登革热是血管内传播和肾上腺感染的四重疾病。结论:登革热与 SWF 之间的关联,与怀孕初期的情况有关。提前诊断并立即干预;这是一部关于卡索的重要的自述。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
沃特豪斯-弗里德里希森综合症 (SWF) 是一种因肾上腺功能不全而导致的疾病,与脑膜炎球菌血症有关,并存在于其他原因之外。登革热和 SWF 的伴随情况非常严重,代表了无法进行全面诊断的情况。
设计与对象
我们对男性患者的临床试验进行了研究,26 岁,目前在二月份,腹部,弥漫性紫癜和脓毒症,演变过程中。对临床、实验室、肉眼观察和组织病理学进行分析,以进行临床医学和尸检检查。
核心方法
通过与实验室相关的宏观、微观和 SWF 病理学结果相关的实验室检查来诊断感染病原体。
主要结果
尸检显示脑膜炎奈瑟菌和 DENV-1 病毒的存在。确认脑膜炎和登革热是血管内传播和肾上腺感染的四重疾病。
解释与创新
登革热与 SWF 之间的关联,是怀孕初期的一个关联。提前诊断并立即干预;这是一部关于卡索的重要的自述。
科研与公卫意义
了解预防登革热和脑膜炎的感染情况的诊断预科和自检的重要性,为预防和治疗复杂疾病提供帮助。
局限与证据强度
我们以这种方式为基础,限制了一般情况。一项证据和证据,可用于进行严格的尸检和实验室检查。

SÍNDROME DE WATERHOUSE-FRIDERICHSEN POR MENINGOCOCCEMIA E COINFECÇÃO COM DENGUE: RELATO DE CASO

Authors: Beatriz Ferreira Cunha, Gabriela Alencar Moura, Sávio Samuel Feitosa Machado, Joel Boechat de Morais Júnior, Joel Boechat de Morais
Original abstract (English)
Introdução: A Síndrome de Waterhouse-Friderichsen (SWF) é uma doença rara caracterizada por insuficiência adrenal, geralmente relacionada à meningococcemia, embora existam outras causas. A ocorrência concomitante de dengue e SWF é extremamente incomum, o que representa um desafio tanto no diagnóstico quanto no tratamento. Objetivo: Relatar um caso de Síndrome de Waterhouse-Friderichsen associada à coinfecção por Neisseria meningitidis e vírus da dengue enfatizando os resultados clínicos, laboratoriais e anatomopatológicos. Metodologia: Foram analisados dados clínicos, laboratoriais, macroscópicos e histopatológicos obtidos de prontuário médico e exame necroscópico realizado pelo Serviço de Verificação de Óbitos de Barbalha (CE). O diagnóstico foi baseado na pesquisa de agentes infecciosos por meio de exames laboratoriais relacionados aos resultados macroscópicos e microscópicos e à fisiopatologia da SWF. Resultados: Paciente masculino, 26 anos, apresentou febre, dor abdominal, púrpura difusa e choque séptico, evoluindo para óbito. O exame post-mortem revelou a presença de Neisseria meningitidis e vírus DENV-1. Esses achados confirmaram coinfecção por meningococo e dengue com quadro de coagulação intravascular disseminada e falência das glândulas adrenais. Conclusão: A associação entre dengue e SWF é rara, porém de alta gravidade. O diagnóstico precoce e a intervenção imediata são vitais; além disso, a autópsia é importante para entender o caso.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
A Síndrome de Waterhouse-Friderichsen (SWF) é uma doença rara caracterizada por insuficiência adrenal, geralmente relacionada à meningococcemia, embora existam outras causas. A ocorrência concomitante de dengue e SWF é extremamente incomum, o que representa um desafio tanto no diagnóstico quanto no tratamento.
Design and population
O estudo relata um caso clínico de um paciente masculino, 26 anos, que apresentou febre, dor abdominal, púrpura difusa e choque séptico, evoluindo para óbito. Foram analisados dados clínicos, laboratoriais, macroscópicos e histopatológicos obtidos de prontuário médico e exame necroscópico.
Core methods
O diagnóstico foi baseado na pesquisa de agentes infecciosos por meio de exames laboratoriais relacionados aos resultados macroscópicos e microscópicos e à fisiopatologia da SWF.
Main results
O exame post-mortem revelou a presença de Neisseria meningitidis e vírus DENV-1. Esses achados confirmaram coinfecção por meningococo e dengue com quadro de coagulação intravascular disseminada e falência das glândulas adrenais.
Interpretation and novelty
A associação entre dengue e SWF é rara, porém de alta gravidade. O diagnóstico precoce e a intervenção imediata são vitais; além disso, a autópsia é importante para entender o caso.
Scientific and public-health significance
Esses achados destacam a importância do diagnóstico precoce e da autópsia para compreender casos de coinfecção entre dengue e meningococo, contribuindo para a vigilância e o manejo clínico de doenças raras e complexas.
Limitations and evidence strength
O estudo é baseado em um único caso, limitando a generalização dos achados. A evidência é de alta confiança, pois foi obtida por meio de exames necroscópicos e laboratoriais rigorosos.

用于登革热 NS1 快速电化学检测的金增强型多电极电化学免疫传感器

Gold-Enhanced Multielectrode Electrochemical Immunosensor for Rapid Electrochemical Detection of Dengue NS1
作者： Luiz Otávio Orzari, Bruna Silva Faria, Jéssica Rocha Camargo, Lucas Aguilera Mariano, Déborah Christine Azzi, Alexandre Gatti, Geisiany Maria de Queiroz, Bruno Campos Janegitz
摘要中文翻译摘要 登革热是世界上最常见的病毒性疾病之一,主要由埃及伊蚊和白纹伊蚊传播。在热带和资源有限的地区,快速、准确的诊断工具至关重要,特别是在以有效控制为目标时,但目前的检测方法在灵敏度、特异性或可及性方面面临限制。在这项工作中,开发了一种多电极一次性电化学免疫传感器,用于检测非结构蛋白 1 (NS1),这是登革热感染的早期生物标志物。该设备提供了四个用金纳米粒子修饰的完全独立且功能齐全的工作电极。这种配置可以在单个平台内进行重复测量并提高再现性,同时使用最小的样品体积。通过实验设计方法优化了金纳米颗粒的电沉积,使系统能够通过基于抗体-抗原识别的特定生物识别架构来适当确定目标蛋白。该生物传感器采用形态学和电化学技术进行表征,实现了 1.0 至 200 ng mL-1 之间的线性响应以及 0.34 ng mL-1 的检测限。随后,添加 NS1 的人血清样本显示回收率在 96.6% 至 115% 之间。所有结果表明,所提出的多电极免疫传感器是一种有前途的、无标记的、便携式 NS1 测定诊断替代方案,同时保持相对较低的成本和简单的制备。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热是世界上最广泛传播的病毒性疾病之一,主要由埃及伊蚊和白纹伊蚊传播。在热带和资源有限的地区,快速、准确的诊断工具至关重要,特别是在以有效控制为目标时,但目前的检测方法在灵敏度、特异性或可及性方面面临限制。
设计与对象
开发了一种多电极一次性电化学免疫传感器,用于检测非结构蛋白 1 (NS1),这是登革热感染的早期生物标志物。该设备提供了四个用金纳米粒子修饰的完全独立且功能齐全的工作电极。通过形态学和电化学技术对生物传感器进行了表征。
核心方法
通过实验设计方法优化了金纳米颗粒的电沉积,使系统能够通过基于抗体-抗原识别的特定生物识别架构来适当确定目标蛋白。添加 NS1 的人血清样本用于评估回收率。
主要结果
该生物传感器在 1.0 至 200 ng mL-1 之间实现了线性响应,检测限为 0.34 ng mL-1。添加 NS1 的人血清样本显示回收率在 96.6% 至 115% 之间。
解释与创新
所提出的多电极免疫传感器是一种有前途的、无标记的、便携式 NS1 测定诊断替代方案,同时保持相对较低的成本和简单的制备。所有结果表明该系统能够高灵敏度和特异性地检测NS1。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,这种生物传感器可以在资源有限的环境中提高登革热感染的快速准确的 诊断,支持更好的疾病控制和管理。它为现有诊断方法提供了潜在的替代方案,具有便携性和成本优势。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象证据,可能无法完全捕捉生物传感器的实际应用或长期性能。由于缺乏详细的实验数据和现实世界的测试,证据强度中等。

Gold-Enhanced Multielectrode Electrochemical Immunosensor for Rapid Electrochemical Detection of Dengue NS1

Authors: Luiz Otávio Orzari, Bruna Silva Faria, Jéssica Rocha Camargo, Lucas Aguilera Mariano, Déborah Christine Azzi, Alexandre Gatti, Geisiany Maria de Queiroz, Bruno Campos Janegitz
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Dengue is one of the most widespread viral diseases in the world, transmitted mainly by Aedes aegypti and Aedes albopictus. In tropical and resource-limited regions, rapid and accurate diagnostic tools are of utmost importance, especially when aiming for effective control, but current detection methods face limitations in sensitivity, specificity, or accessibility. In this work, a multielectrode disposable electrochemical immunosensor was developed for the detection of the nonstructural protein 1 (NS1), an early biomarker of dengue infection. This device presents four fully individual and functional working electrodes modified with gold nanoparticles. This configuration enables replicate measurements and improved reproducibility within a single platform while using minimal sample volume. The electrodeposition of Au nanoparticles was optimized through Design of Experiments approaches, allowing the system to suitably determine the target protein with a specific biorecognition architecture based on antibody–antigen recognition. This biosensor was characterized by morphological and electrochemical techniques, achieving a linear response between 1.0 and 200 ng mL–1 and a limit of detection of 0.34 ng mL–1. Afterward, human serum samples spiked with NS1 showed recovery rates between 96.6% and 115%. All results indicate that the proposed multielectrode immunosensor is a promising, label-free, and portable diagnostic alternative for NS1 determination while maintaining a relatively low cost and simple preparation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue is one of the most widespread viral diseases in the world, transmitted mainly by Aedes aegypti and Aedes albopictus. In tropical and resource-limited regions, rapid and accurate diagnostic tools are of utmost importance, especially when aiming for effective control, but current detection methods face limitations in sensitivity, specificity, or accessibility.
Design and population
A multielectrode disposable electrochemical immunosensor was developed for the detection of the nonstructural protein 1 (NS1), an early biomarker of dengue infection. This device presents four fully individual and functional working electrodes modified with gold nanoparticles. The biosensor was characterized by morphological and electrochemical techniques.
Core methods
The electrodeposition of Au nanoparticles was optimized through Design of Experiments approaches, allowing the system to suitably determine the target protein with a specific biorecognition architecture based on antibody–antigen recognition. Human serum samples spiked with NS1 were used to evaluate recovery rates.
Main results
The biosensor achieved a linear response between 1.0 and 200 ng mL–1 and a limit of detection of 0.34 ng mL–1. Human serum samples spiked with NS1 showed recovery rates between 96.6% and 115%.
Interpretation and novelty
The proposed multielectrode immunosensor is a promising, label-free, and portable diagnostic alternative for NS1 determination while maintaining a relatively low cost and simple preparation. All results indicate that the system is capable of detecting NS1 with high sensitivity and specificity.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that this biosensor could improve the rapid and accurate diagnosis of dengue infection in resource-limited settings, supporting better disease control and management. It provides a potential alternative to existing diagnostic methods with advantages in portability and cost.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-only evidence, which may not fully capture the practical application or long-term performance of the biosensor. The evidence strength is moderate due to the lack of detailed experimental data and real-world testing.

AG129小鼠登革热感染途径对疾病进展和免疫应答影响的初步研究

A pilot study on the impact of infection routes on disease progression and immune response by dengue infection in AG129 mice
作者： Nur Hana Md Jelas, Nor Azrina Norahmad, Tiffiny Chau Dee Ho, Bazilah Jusoh, Umi Rubiah Sastu, Murizal Zainol, Ravindran Thayan, Ami Fazlin Syed Mohamed, Mohd Ridzuan Mohd Abd Razak
摘要中文翻译AG129小鼠是登革热发病机制研究中常用的小鼠模型,采用各种病毒接种途径。不同的病毒感染途径可能会引起不同的 宿主 反应。该初步研究比较了 AG129 小鼠通过腹膜内和皮下途径感染 登革病毒 的情况。AG129小鼠通过腹膜内或皮下注射感染登革病毒 2型(DENV-2)。通过每日体重和发病评分监测疾病进展,持续 14 天。通过逆转录定量PCR对血浆病毒RNA水平进行定量。采用登革热 NS1 抗原 ELISA 法测定血浆非结构蛋白 1 (NS1) 水平。计算血小板和血液分类计数。使用多重免疫测定在感染后第 1、3、5 和 7 天评估血浆细胞因子。直到感染后第 4 天,各组之间的体重下降相似,其中腹膜内感染的小鼠表现出更大的下降。感染后第 2 天至第 6 天,腹腔内感染的小鼠的发病迹象更为明显。NS1 蛋白和病毒 RNA 检测到的病毒血症水平在两种途径之间相似。腹膜内感染引发白细胞和中性粒细胞计数较早增加,以及淋巴细胞较早减少和血小板减少。细胞因子分析显示腹腔内感染小鼠中七种细胞因子的早期升高。相比之下,皮下感染的小鼠显示出较高水平的干扰素-γ和白细胞介素-10。与皮下途径相比,AG129 小鼠腹膜内感染 DENV-2 会诱导血小板减少症、免疫应答 发生更早,并且发病率短暂升高。因此,在未来使用 AG129 小鼠模型的实验设计中应仔细考虑不同的病毒接种途径。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查不同的感染途径如何影响 登革病毒 感染中的疾病进展和 免疫应答,因为不同的病毒接种途径可能会引发不同的 宿主 反应。
设计与对象
对通过腹膜内或皮下途径感染 登革病毒 2 型 (DENV-2) 的 AG129 小鼠进行了初步研究,以比较 登革病毒 感染的发展情况。
核心方法
通过逆转录定量PCR对血浆病毒RNA水平进行定量,并通过登革热NS1抗原ELISA方法测定血浆非结构蛋白1(NS1)水平。使用多重免疫测定在感染后第 1、3、5 和 7 天评估血浆细胞因子。
主要结果
直到感染后第 4 天,各组之间的体重下降相似,其中腹膜内感染的小鼠表现出更大的下降。感染后第 2 天至第 6 天,腹腔内感染的小鼠的发病迹象更为明显。细胞因子分析显示,腹腔内感染的小鼠中七种细胞因子的早期升高,而皮下感染的小鼠则显示出较高水平的干扰素-γ和白细胞介素-10。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,与皮下途径相比,AG129小鼠腹腔内感染DENV-2会引起血小板减少症、更早的免疫应答和短暂较高的发病率,这凸显了感染途径在疾病进展中的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可以为未来使用 AG129 小鼠模型的实验设计提供信息,以更好地理解 登革病毒 发病机制 并有可能改进疾病预防和 治疗 的策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究是一项试点研究,样本量有限,证据仅基于摘要数据,可能无法完全反映 免疫应答 和疾病进展的复杂性。这些发现的可信度是中等的。

A pilot study on the impact of infection routes on disease progression and immune response by dengue infection in AG129 mice

Authors: Nur Hana Md Jelas, Nor Azrina Norahmad, Tiffiny Chau Dee Ho, Bazilah Jusoh, Umi Rubiah Sastu, Murizal Zainol, Ravindran Thayan, Ami Fazlin Syed Mohamed, Mohd Ridzuan Mohd Abd Razak
Original abstract (English)
AG129 mouse is a mouse model commonly utilised in the study of dengue pathogenesis using various viral inoculation routes. Different viral infection routes may elicit distinct host responses. This pilot study compares the development of dengue virus infection in AG129 mice via intraperitoneal and subcutaneous routes. AG129 mice were infected with dengue virus type 2 (DENV-2) via intraperitoneal or subcutaneous injection. Disease progression was monitored through daily body weight and morbidity scores for 14 days. Plasma viral RNA levels were quantified by reverse transcription-quantitative PCR. Plasma non-structural protein 1 (NS1) level was determined by dengue NS1 antigen- ELISA method. Platelet and blood differential counts were counted. Plasma cytokines were assessed on day 1, 3, 5, and 7 post-infection using multiplex immunoassay. Body weight loss was similar between groups until day 4 post-infection, where intraperitoneally infected mice showed greater loss. Morbidity signs were more pronounced in intraperitoneally infected mice from day 2 to 6 post-infection. Viremia levels detected by NS1 protein and viral RNA were similar between both routes. Intraperitoneal infection triggered an earlier increase in white blood cells and neutrophil counts, as well as earlier lymphocyte reduction and thrombocytopenia. Cytokine analysis revealed early elevations of seven cytokines in intraperitoneally infected mice. In contrast, subcutaneously infected mice showed higher levels of interferon-γ and interleukin-10. Intraperitoneal infection in AG129 mice with DENV-2 induced thrombocytopenia, earlier immune responses and transiently higher morbidity as compared to the subcutaneous route. Therefore, different routes of virus inoculation should be carefully considered in future experimental designs using the AG129 mouse model.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate how different infection routes affect disease progression and immune response in dengue virus infections, as distinct host responses may be elicited by various viral inoculation routes.
Design and population
A pilot study was conducted on AG129 mice infected with dengue virus type 2 (DENV-2) via intraperitoneal or subcutaneous routes to compare the development of dengue virus infection.
Core methods
Plasma viral RNA levels were quantified by reverse transcription-quantitative PCR, and plasma non-structural protein 1 (NS1) levels were determined by dengue NS1 antigen-ELISA method. Plasma cytokines were assessed on day 1, 3, 5, and 7 post-infection using multiplex immunoassay.
Main results
Body weight loss was similar between groups until day 4 post-infection, where intraperitoneally infected mice showed greater loss. Morbidity signs were more pronounced in intraperitoneally infected mice from day 2 to 6 post-infection. Cytokine analysis revealed early elevations of seven cytokines in intraperitoneally infected mice, while subcutaneously infected mice showed higher levels of interferon-γ and interleukin-10.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that intraperitoneal infection in AG129 mice with DENV-2 induces thrombocytopenia, earlier immune responses and transiently higher morbidity compared to the subcutaneous route, highlighting the importance of infection route in disease progression.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results can inform future experimental designs using the AG129 mouse model to better understand dengue virus pathogenesis and potentially improve strategies for disease prevention and treatment.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is a pilot study with limited sample size, and the evidence is based on abstract-only data, which may not fully capture the complexity of immune responses and disease progression. The confidence in these findings is moderate.

埃及伊蚊无法控制的数字平台:USUÁRIOS MUNICÍPios 登革热事件探索平台 (2023-2026) 埃及伊蚊控制数字平台:使用巴西南里约热内卢州登革热零平台对各城市的登革热 发病率 进行探索性评估(2023-2026) 埃及伊蚊控制平台:EVALUACIÓN EXPLORATORIA DE LA里约热内卢州南部平台登革热零事件 (2023–2026)

PLATAFORMAS DIGITAIS NO CONTROLE DO AEDES AEGYPTI: AVALIAÇÃO EXPLORATÓRIA DA INCIDÊNCIA DE DENGUE EM MUNICÍPIOS USUÁRIOS DA PLATAFORMA DENGUE ZERO NO SUL FLUMINENSE (2023–2026) DIGITAL PLATFORMS FOR AEDES AEGYPTI CONTROL: AN EXPLORATORY ASSESSMENT OF DENGUE INCIDENCE IN MUNICIPALITIES USING THE DENGUE ZERO PLATFORM IN SOUTHERN RIO DE JANEIRO STATE, BRAZIL (2023–2026) PLATAFORMAS DIGITALES EN EL CONTROL DE AEDES AEGYPTI: EVALUACIÓN EXPLORATORIA DE LA INCIDENCIA DEL DENGUE EN MUNICIPIOS USUARIOS DE LA PLATAFORMA DENGUE ZERO EN EL SUR DEL ESTADO DE RÍO DE JANEIRO (2023–2026)
作者： Carlos José Ruffato Favoreto, Georgia Felicio Marinho da Silva, Janice Rezende Vieira Peixoto
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热是巴西公共主要问题的永久性问题,是 2024 年的流行病,是国家历史上的重大历史事件,是精确控制埃及伊蚊的技术手段的强化。
设计与对象
我们利用“零登革热”平台分析了弗鲁米尼塞市登革热事件的演变,解决了无人机图像关联问题、东方计算和地理参考问题,以及防治流行病的工作人员,与市政当局进行比较。
核心方法
生态研究、探索和描述,是 InfoDengue 系统的第二个基础,将在 2023 年和 2026 年期间提供,将安格拉多斯雷斯和巴拉曼萨与帕拉蒂、沃尔塔雷东达和雷森德等城市进行比较。
主要结果
Foram calculadas taxas de incidência por 100 mil habitantes, razões de taxas e razões entre razões de taxas.
解释与创新
作为 2025 年安格拉杜斯雷斯和 2025 年 4 月巴拉曼萨发烧植入平台,2023 年和 2024 年之前的干预期,2025 年植入期和 2026 年初期完成功能。
科研与公卫意义
Em 2026, o município registrou a maior redução entre os municípios analisados (93,8%), queda proporcionalmente superior à dos comparadores, a menor incidência de toda a série e nenhuma semana acima do nível amarelo de alerta, enquanto Barra Mansa acompanhou a redução de seus vizinhos.
局限与证据强度
获得的结果是,我们对前面的免疫进行了解释,并为平台做出了贡献,并将其与旋转结合在一起。

PLATAFORMAS DIGITAIS NO CONTROLE DO AEDES AEGYPTI: AVALIAÇÃO EXPLORATÓRIA DA INCIDÊNCIA DE DENGUE EM MUNICÍPIOS USUÁRIOS DA PLATAFORMA DENGUE ZERO NO SUL FLUMINENSE (2023–2026) DIGITAL PLATFORMS FOR AEDES AEGYPTI CONTROL: AN EXPLORATORY ASSESSMENT OF DENGUE INCIDENCE IN MUNICIPALITIES USING THE DENGUE ZERO PLATFORM IN SOUTHERN RIO DE JANEIRO STATE, BRAZIL (2023–2026) PLATAFORMAS DIGITALES EN EL CONTROL DE AEDES AEGYPTI: EVALUACIÓN EXPLORATORIA DE LA INCIDENCIA DEL DENGUE EN MUNICIPIOS USUARIOS DE LA PLATAFORMA DENGUE ZERO EN EL SUR DEL ESTADO DE RÍO DE JANEIRO (2023–2026)

Authors: Carlos José Ruffato Favoreto, Georgia Felicio Marinho da Silva, Janice Rezende Vieira Peixoto
Original abstract (English)
A dengue permanece entre os principais problemas de saúde pública no Brasil, e a epidemia de 2024, a maior da série histórica nacional, intensificou a procura por tecnologias capazes de tornar o controle do Aedes aegypti mais preciso. Este artigo analisa a evolução da incidência de dengue em municípios do sul fluminense que utilizam a plataforma Dengue Zero, solução que associa imagens obtidas por drones, visão computacional e georreferenciamento para orientar o trabalho dos agentes de combate às endemias, comparando-a com a de municípios vizinhos. Trata-se de estudo ecológico, exploratório e descritivo, baseado em dados secundários do sistema InfoDengue, com casos prováveis entre 2023 e 2026, tendo Angra dos Reis e Barra Mansa como municípios de interesse e Paraty, Volta Redonda e Resende como comparadores. Foram calculadas taxas de incidência por 100 mil habitantes, razões de taxas e razões entre razões de taxas. Como a plataforma foi implantada em fevereiro de 2025 em Angra dos Reis e em abril de 2025 em Barra Mansa, 2023 e 2024 correspondem ao período anterior à intervenção, 2025 à fase de implantação e 2026 à primeira temporada completa de funcionamento. Em 2025, a onda epidêmica de Angra dos Reis já estava em curso quando a plataforma começou a operar. Em 2026, o município registrou a maior redução entre os municípios analisados (93,8%), queda proporcionalmente superior à dos comparadores, a menor incidência de toda a série e nenhuma semana acima do nível amarelo de alerta, enquanto Barra Mansa acompanhou a redução de seus vizinhos. Esses resultados, embora devam ser interpretados à luz da imunidade adquirida nas ondas anteriores, são compatíveis com uma contribuição da plataforma após sua plena incorporação à rotina. Conclui-se que a plataforma Dengue Zero representa um modelo promissor de inovação no controle vetorial, ao associar drones, inteligência artificial e georreferenciamento para alcançar criadouros que escapam à inspeção convencional e tornar o trabalho de campo mais preciso. Sua expansão para outros bairros, municípios e estados é recomendável e, se acompanhada de séries temporais e de indicadores operacionais, poderá consolidar as evidências de sua contribuição para o controle da dengue.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
A dengue permanece entre os principais problemas de saúde pública no Brasil, e a epidemia de 2024, a maior da série histórica nacional, intensificou a procura por tecnologias capazes de tornar o controle do Aedes aegypti mais preciso.

Trata-se de estudo ecológico, exploratório e descritivo, baseado em dados secundários do sistema InfoDengue, com casos prováveis entre 2023 e 2026, tendo Angra dos Reis e Barra Mansa como municípios de interesse e Paraty, Volta Redonda e Resende como comparadores.
Main results
Foram calculadas taxas de incidência por 100 mil habitantes, razões de taxas e razões entre razões de taxas.

Como a plataforma foi implantada em fevereiro de 2025 em Angra dos Reis e em abril de 2025 em Barra Mansa, 2023 e 2024 correspondem ao período anterior à intervenção, 2025 à fase de implantação e 2026 à primeira temporada completa de funcionamento.
Scientific and public-health significance
Em 2026, o município registrou a maior redução entre os municípios analisados (93,8%), queda proporcionalmente superior à dos comparadores, a menor incidência de toda a série e nenhuma semana acima do nível amarelo de alerta, enquanto Barra Mansa acompanhou a redução de seus vizinhos.

Esses resultados, embora devam ser interpretados à luz da imunidade adquirida nas ondas anteriores, são compatíveis com uma contribuição da plataforma após sua plena incorporação à rotina.

登革热疾病传播模型的全局特性及最优控制研究

A study of the global properties and optimal control of dengue disease transmission model
作者： Harouna Ouedraogo, Dramane Ouedraogo, Idrissa Ibrango, Aboudramane Guiro
摘要中文翻译在这项工作中,我们制定了一个非线性微分方程组,对 登革热 的 传播 动力学进行建模。该模型表现出经典的阈值行为。我们证明,当基本再生数小于 1 时,无病平衡局部和全局渐近稳定;当基本再生数大于 1 时,地方性平衡在一定条件下全局渐近稳定。平衡的稳定性是通过利用李雅普诺夫稳定性理论和拉萨尔不变原理推导出来的。此外,我们还制定了一个最优控制问题,涉及预防措施、易感人群疫苗接种的投资以及与杀虫剂治疗相关的投资。提供数值模拟来说明理论结果。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在了解登革热疾病 传播 的整体特性,并探索其预防和管理的最佳控制策略。
设计与对象
该研究涉及建立一个数学模型来表示 登革热 的 传播 动力学,重点关注不同条件下无病和地方性平衡的稳定性。
核心方法
利用Lyapunov稳定性理论和LaSalle不变原理对模型进行分析,根据基本再生数确定平衡的稳定性。
主要结果
该模型表明,在一定条件下,当基本繁殖数小于1时,无病平衡是稳定的;当基本繁殖数超过1时,地方病平衡是稳定的。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为理解登革热 传播 动力学的理论基础,并提出了涉及 疫苗接种 和杀虫剂 治疗 的最佳控制策略。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为登革热 传播 的数学模型提供了见解,可以为疾病控制和预防的公共卫生策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于理论分析和数值模拟,可能无法完全捕捉现实世界的复杂性,但证据确凿且基于数学原理。

A study of the global properties and optimal control of dengue disease transmission model

Authors: Harouna Ouedraogo, Dramane Ouedraogo, Idrissa Ibrango, Aboudramane Guiro
Original abstract (English)
In this work, we formulate a nonlinear system of differential equations that models the transmission dynamics of dengue fever. The model exhibits the classical threshold behavior. We prove that when the basic reproduction number is less than one, the disease-free equilibrium is locally and globally asymptotically stable and when the basic reproduction number is greater than one the endemic equilibrium is globally asymptotically stable under certain conditions. The stability of equilibria is derived through the use of Lyapunov stability theory and LaSalle's invariance principle. In addition we formulate an optimal control problem involving preventive measures, the investment in vaccination of susceptible humans and the investment related to insecticide treatment. Numerical simulations are provided to illustrate the theoretical results.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to understand the global properties of dengue disease transmission and explore optimal control strategies for its prevention and management.
Design and population
The study involved the formulation of a mathematical model to represent the transmission dynamics of dengue fever, focusing on the stability of disease-free and endemic equilibria under different conditions.
Core methods
The model was analyzed using Lyapunov stability theory and LaSalle's invariance principle to determine the stability of equilibria based on the basic reproduction number.
Main results
The model demonstrated that the disease-free equilibrium is stable when the basic reproduction number is less than one, and the endemic equilibrium is stable when it exceeds one, under certain conditions.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as a theoretical foundation for understanding dengue transmission dynamics and proposed optimal control strategies involving vaccination and insecticide treatment.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide insights into the mathematical modeling of dengue transmission, which can inform public health strategies for disease control and prevention.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on theoretical analysis and numerical simulations, which may not fully capture real-world complexities, but the evidence is strong and grounded in mathematical principles.

厄瓜多尔气候气候协会预防登革热的社区责任

Rol de enfermería comunitaria en la prevención del dengue ante factores asociados al cambio climático en Ecuador
作者： Carmen De Las Mercedes Cevallos Mendez, Genesis Jazmin Ruiz Zerna, Damaris Gabriela Sopalo Moposita, Mical Elisa Pullay Moyolema, Carmen de las Mercedes Cevallos Méndez
摘要中文翻译我们对厄瓜多尔传播过程中发生的气候和社会环境因素对登革热预防社区环境的作用进行了分析。为此,我们对有关登革热、气候变化、卫生教育、治安和控制社区的科学文献和技术文献的相关文献登记和补充的综合叙述进行了修订。我们优先考虑在厄瓜多尔实现的研究,特别是在沿海地区,并结合国际上的证据进行比较。登革热的传播与温度、降水、厄尔尼诺现象、生活条件、水的相互作用以及国内基本服务和实践的相互作用有关。在这种情况下,社区的存在包括卫生教育、国家识别、领土警戒、坦普拉纳检测、社会运动和矢量控制行动的解决。干预措施的结果是可以适应当地的特征和连续性。最后,我们要加强预防、预测、区域和部门间信息的整合,整合气候信息、流行病学和社区参与,为响应者提供当地传播情况的机会。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
我们对厄瓜多尔传播过程中发生的气候和社会环境因素对登革热预防社区环境的作用进行了分析。
设计与对象
我们对有关登革热、气候变化、卫生教育、治安和社区控制的科学文献和技术文献进行了修订和综合叙述。
核心方法
我们优先考虑在厄瓜多尔实现的研究,特别是在沿海地区,并结合国际上的证据进行比较。
主要结果
登革热的传播与温度、降水、厄尔尼诺现象、生活条件、水的相互作用以及国内基本服务和实践的相互作用有关。
解释与创新
在这种情况下,社区的存在包括卫生教育、国家识别、领土警戒、坦普拉纳检测、社会运动和矢量控制行动的解决。
科研与公卫意义
干预措施的结果是可以适应当地的特征和连续性。
局限与证据强度
最后,我们要加强预防、预测、区域和部门间信息的整合,整合气候信息、流行病学和社区参与,为响应者提供当地传播情况的机会。

Rol de enfermería comunitaria en la prevención del dengue ante factores asociados al cambio climático en Ecuador

Authors: Carmen De Las Mercedes Cevallos Mendez, Genesis Jazmin Ruiz Zerna, Damaris Gabriela Sopalo Moposita, Mical Elisa Pullay Moyolema, Carmen de las Mercedes Cevallos Méndez
Original abstract (English)
Este estudio analiza el rol de la enfermería comunitaria en la prevención del dengue frente a los factores climáticos y socio-ambientales que inciden en el riesgo de transmisión en Ecuador. Para ello, se desarrolló una revisión narrativa integrativa basada en los registros bibliográficos proporcionados y complementada con literatura científica y documentos técnicos vinculados con dengue, cambio climático, educación sanitaria, vigilancia y control comunitario. Se priorizaron estudios realizados en Ecuador, especialmente en zonas costeras, y se incorporó evidencia internacional de enfermería únicamente con fines comparativos. Los hallazgos muestran que la transmisión del dengue está asociada con la interacción entre temperatura, precipitaciones, variabilidad relacionada con El Niño, condiciones de vivienda, almacenamiento de agua, acceso a servicios básicos y prácticas domésticas. En este contexto, la enfermería comunitaria cumple un papel articulador mediante la educación sanitaria, la identificación de riesgos, la vigilancia territorial, la detección temprana, la movilización social y el seguimiento de acciones de control vectorial. Las intervenciones resultan más sostenibles cuando se adaptan a las características locales y mantienen continuidad. Se concluye que la enfermería debe fortalecer un enfoque preventivo, anticipatorio, territorial e intersectorial, integrado con información climática, vigilancia epidemiológica y participación comunitaria para responder oportunamente a escenarios cambiantes de transmisión local.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Este estudio analiza el rol de la enfermería comunitaria en la prevención del dengue frente a los factores climáticos y socio-ambientales que inciden en el riesgo de transmisión en Ecuador.
Design and population
Se desarrolló una revisión narrativa integrativa basada en los registros bibliográficos proporcionados y complementada con literatura científica y documentos técnicos vinculados con dengue, cambio climático, educación sanitaria, vigilancia y control comunitario.
