寨卡病毒非结构蛋白 1 的成熟和分泌需要脯氨酰羟基化

Prolyl hydroxylation is required for maturation and secretion of Zika virus non-structural protein 1
作者： Bodine Bezemer, Gijs J. Overheul, Milou Stevens, Cornelia G. Spruijt, Michiel Vermeulen, Pascal Miesen, Ronald P. van Rij
摘要中文翻译摘要 蚊媒正黄病毒,如寨卡病毒 (ZIKV) 和 登革病毒 (DENV) 具有很高的流行潜力,并与多种可能的结果相关,从无症状感染到严重并发症。正黄病毒非结构蛋白 1 (NS1) 可以是膜结合的,也可以从细胞中分泌,在病毒复制、免疫逃避和 发病机制 中发挥重要作用。为了描述 ZIKV NS1 的相互作用组,我们用表达 FLAG 标签 NS1 的重组 ZIKV 感染 Huh7 和神经祖细胞,并通过定量质谱法鉴定相互作用蛋白。在这两种细胞类型中,多种脯氨酰羟化酶、糖基化酶、ER 相关降解蛋白和参与免疫的蛋白质被鉴定为 NS1 相互作用蛋白。质谱数据显示NS1含有潜在的羟脯氨酸残基,表明脯氨酰羟化酶参与了NS1的翻译后修饰。一致认为,脯氨酰羟化酶的化学抑制会损害 NS1 的细胞内运输和质膜定位,并降低 ZIKV RNA 水平和滴度。281位脯氨酸残基的诱变损害了NS1的分泌,但病毒复制不受影响。此外,在第 267 位取代脯氨酸会严重失调 NS1 表达,并在单次传代后恢复为野生型序列,表明该 突变 会导致严重的复制缺陷。我们的结果确定脯氨酰羟基化是 ZIKV NS1 的重要翻译后修饰。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在研究脯氨酰羟基化在寨卡病毒非结构蛋白 1 成熟和分泌中的作用,因为寨卡病毒和 DENV 等蚊媒正黄病毒具有显着的流行潜力和不同的临床结果。
设计与对象
该研究涉及用表达 FLAG 标签 NS1 的重组 ZIKV 感染 Huh7 和神经祖细胞,以描述 ZIKV NS1 的相互作用组。
核心方法
使用定量质谱法来鉴定 ZIKV NS1 的相互作用蛋白,并利用脯氨酰羟化酶的化学抑制来评估它们在 NS1 运输和病毒 RNA 水平中的作用。
主要结果
质谱数据表明 NS1 含有潜在的羟脯氨酸残基,脯氨酰羟化酶的化学抑制降低了 ZIKV RNA 水平和滴度。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,脯氨酰羟基化对于 ZIKV NS1 的翻译后修饰至关重要,这对其功能至关重要。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果提供了对 ZIKV NS1 分子机制的深入了解,并可能为未来病毒复制和 发病机制 的研究提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外实验,由于缺乏体内验证,证据不充分。

Prolyl hydroxylation is required for maturation and secretion of Zika virus non-structural protein 1

Authors: Bodine Bezemer, Gijs J. Overheul, Milou Stevens, Cornelia G. Spruijt, Michiel Vermeulen, Pascal Miesen, Ronald P. van Rij
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Mosquito-borne orthoflaviviruses such as Zika virus (ZIKV) and dengue virus (DENV) have a high epidemic potential and are associated with a wide range of possible outcomes, from asymptomatic infections to severe complications. The orthoflavivirus non-structural protein 1 (NS1), which can be membrane-bound as well as secreted from the cell, plays important roles in viral replication, immune evasion, and pathogenesis. To describe the interactome of ZIKV NS1, we infected Huh7 and neural progenitor cells with a recombinant ZIKV expressing FLAG-tagged NS1 and identified interacting proteins by quantitative mass spectrometry. In both cell types, multiple prolyl hydroxylases, glycosylation enzymes, ER-associated degradation proteins, and proteins involved in immunity were identified as NS1 interactors. Mass spectrometry data showed that NS1 contains potential hydroxyproline residues, suggesting that prolyl hydroxylase enzymes are involved in post-translational modification of NS1. In agreement, chemical inhibition of prolyl hydroxylases impaired NS1 intracellular trafficking and plasma membrane localization and reduced ZIKV RNA levels and titers. Mutagenesis of the proline residue at position 281 impaired NS1 secretion, although viral replication was unaffected. Moreover, substituting proline at position 267 strongly dysregulated NS1 expression and reverted to the wild-type sequence already after a single passage, suggesting that this mutation leads to severe replication defects. Our results identify prolyl hydroxylation as an essential post-translational modification of ZIKV NS1.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the role of prolyl hydroxylation in the maturation and secretion of Zika virus non-structural protein 1, as mosquito-borne orthoflaviviruses like ZIKV and DENV have significant epidemic potential and diverse clinical outcomes.
Design and population
The study involved infecting Huh7 and neural progenitor cells with a recombinant ZIKV expressing FLAG-tagged NS1 to describe the interactome of ZIKV NS1.
Core methods
Quantitative mass spectrometry was used to identify interacting proteins of ZIKV NS1, and chemical inhibition of prolyl hydroxylases was employed to assess their role in NS1 trafficking and viral RNA levels.
Main results
Mass spectrometry data indicated that NS1 contains potential hydroxyproline residues, and chemical inhibition of prolyl hydroxylases reduced ZIKV RNA levels and titers.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that prolyl hydroxylation is essential for the post-translational modification of ZIKV NS1, which is critical for its function.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide insights into the molecular mechanisms of ZIKV NS1 and may inform future research on viral replication and pathogenesis.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro experiments, and the evidence is moderate due to the absence of in vivo validation.

一项全国性生态时间序列分析发现,2020 年至 2025 年墨西哥登革热和 COVID-19 监测 记录之间没有稳定的时间或预测关系

A nationwide ecological time-series analysis found no stable temporal or predictive relationship between dengue and COVID-19 surveillance records in Mexico from 2020 to 2025
作者： Igor Garcia-Atutxa, Carmen Luna Cruz-Herrero, Francisca Villanueva-Flores
摘要中文翻译这项研究发现 2020 年至 2025 年墨西哥登革热和 COVID-19 监测 记录之间没有稳定的时间或预测关系,这表明 COVID-19 动态不会增强登革热 监测 的预测。我们分析了 1,085,869 条登革热记录和 20,632,470 条 COVID-19 记录。这些发现支持两个 监测 系列之间不存在稳定的直接时间或预测耦合;汇总逆关联主要是日历时间和流行病制度结构的特征。因此,本研究没有测试或推断 登革病毒 和 SARS-CoV-2 之间的免疫相互作用。之前的一项多国研究提出了一种连接登革热和 COVID-19 的神经网络“相关模型”,同时纳入了气候、假期和时间结构等外部变量 [28]。在本研究中,我们使用个人级别的 监测 记录将这种建模逻辑扩展到墨西哥,这些记录在大流行期间和大流行后时期汇总到联邦实体周面板中。文章摘要 COVID-19 大流行对传染病的生态和 监测 产生了重大影响,引发了有关登革热和 COVID-19 之间潜在相互作用的问题。摘要 使用国家 监测 记录,我们评估了 COVID-19 动态是否解释、预测或改进了 2020-2025 年墨西哥每周登革热 监测 记录负担的预测。对每日症状发作覆盖率的审计发现,有 10 个不完整的登革热周集中在年末/源快照边界周围;这些周被视为缺失而不是零,留下来自 32 个实体的 8,695 个州周观察结果用于推断分析。主要每周计数转换为 log10(记录 + 1),并使用固定 202 进行人口标准化敏感性分析...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估鉴于两种疾病之间潜在的生态和 监测 相互作用,COVID-19 大流行的动态是否可以解释、预测或改进 2020-2025 年墨西哥登革热 监测 的预测。
设计与对象
使用墨西哥登革热和 COVID-19 的国家 监测 记录进行了一项纵向生态研究,涵盖 2020 年至 2025 年期间。实体代码 1-32 在两个数据集中都是匹配的,并包含在配对的登革热 - COVID-19 面板中。
核心方法
该研究使用了国家 监测 记录,并使用分类流行病学年份、流行病学周的循环三次样条和连续时间的三次回归样条分别在每个州内残留登革热和 COVID-19。每周 监测 记录计数转换为 log10(率 + 1)以进行群体标准化分析,并通过精确的 7 天日历匹配创建滞后变量,以避免人为滞后链。
主要结果
该研究发现登革热和 COVID-19 监测 记录之间存在皮尔逊反相关性(r = -0.331,bootstrap 95% CI -0.349 至 -0.312;Spearman rho = -0.311),但这种关联的方向和幅度因年份而异,从 2021 年的 r = 0.096 到 2023 年的 r = -0.312。人口标准化得出 r = -0.386,而消除州和日历周效应则将关联性降低至 r = -0.049。
解释与创新
作者解释说,缺乏稳定的时间或预测关系表明,大流行的动态不会增强登革热 监测 的预测,并且他们使用汇总到联邦实体周面板中的个人级 监测 记录将建模逻辑扩展到墨西哥。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,墨西哥的登革热和 COVID-19 的 监测 系统并未为预测登革热 监测 提供稳定的基础,这对于将 监测 数据整合到公共卫生规划中以及了解大流行期间疾病相互作用具有影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究承认,由于季节性、长期趋势、地理异质性、报告假象以及登革热自身的自回归行为,每周的生态相关性可能会产生误导。证据是中等的,因为分析因缺乏 2020 年之前的基线以及使用汇总数据而不是个人层面的风险而受到限制。

A nationwide ecological time-series analysis found no stable temporal or predictive relationship between dengue and COVID-19 surveillance records in Mexico from 2020 to 2025

Authors: Igor Garcia-Atutxa, Carmen Luna Cruz-Herrero, Francisca Villanueva-Flores
Original abstract (English)
This study found no stable temporal or predictive relationship between dengue and COVID-19 surveillance records in Mexico from 2020 to 2025, indicating that COVID-19 dynamics do not enhance the prediction of dengue incidence. We analysed 1,085,869 dengue records and 20,632,470 COVID-19 records. These findings support no stable direct temporal or predictive coupling between the two surveillance series; the pooled inverse association is primarily a feature of calendar-time and epidemic-regime structure. Accordingly, the present study does not test or infer immunological interactions between dengue virus and SARS-CoV-2. A previous multi-country study proposed a neural-network “Correlation Model” linking dengue and COVID-19 while incorporating external variables such as climate, holidays, and temporal structure [28]. In this study, we extend this modelling logic to Mexico using individual-level surveillance records aggregated into a federal-entity–week panel across the pandemic and post-pandemic period. Article summary The COVID-19 pandemic significantly impacted the ecology and surveillance of infectious diseases, raising questions about potential interactions between dengue and COVID-19. Abstract Using national surveillance records, we evaluated whether COVID-19 dynamics explain, anticipate, or improve prediction of weekly dengue surveillance-record burden in Mexico during 2020–2025. Audit of daily symptom-onset coverage identified 10 incomplete dengue weeks clustered around year-end/source snapshot boundaries; these weeks were treated as missing rather than zero, leaving 8,695 state-week observations from 32 entities for inferential analyses. Primary weekly counts were transformed as log10(records + 1), with a population-standardized sensitivity analysis using fixed 202...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate whether the dynamics of the COVID-19 pandemic could explain, anticipate, or improve the prediction of dengue incidence in Mexico during 2020–2025, given the potential ecological and surveillance interactions between these two diseases.
Design and population
A longitudinal ecological study was conducted using national surveillance records for dengue and COVID-19 in Mexico, covering the period from 2020 to 2025. Entity codes 1–32 were matchable across both datasets and were included in the paired dengue–COVID-19 panel.
Core methods
The study used national surveillance records and residualized dengue and COVID-19 separately within each state using categorical epidemiological year, a cyclic cubic spline for epidemiological week, and a cubic regression spline for continuous time. Weekly surveillance-record counts were transformed as log10(rate + 1) for population-standardized analyses, and lag variables were created by exact 7-day calendar matching to avoid artificial lag chains.
Main results
The study found an inverse Pearson correlation (r = -0.331, bootstrap 95% CI -0.349 to -0.312; Spearman rho = -0.311) between dengue and COVID-19 surveillance records, but the direction and magnitude of this association varied by year, from r = 0.096 in 2021 to r = -0.312 in 2023. Population standardization yielded r = -0.386, whereas removal of state and calendar-week effects reduced the association to r = -0.049.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that the lack of a stable temporal or predictive relationship suggests that the dynamics of the pandemic do not enhance the prediction of dengue incidence, and they extended the modelling logic to Mexico using individual-level surveillance records aggregated into a federal-entity–week panel.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that the surveillance systems for dengue and COVID-19 in Mexico do not provide a stable basis for predicting dengue incidence, which has implications for the integration of surveillance data in public health planning and the understanding of disease interactions during pandemics.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study acknowledges that weekly ecological correlations may be misleading due to seasonality, secular trends, geographic heterogeneity, reporting artefacts, and dengue’s own autoregressive behavior. The evidence is moderate, as the analysis was limited by the absence of a pre-2020 baseline and the use of aggregated data rather than individual-level risk.

在印度尼西亚巴鲁开发带有 WhatsApp 通知的基于网络的登革热天气警报原型并进行功能测试

Development and Functional Testing of a Web Based Dengue Weather Alert Prototype with WhatsApp Notifications in Palu, Indonesia
作者： Iqbal Hidayat Syahril, Kurnia Hayat Hag, Diah Mutiarasari, Rizan Machmud
摘要中文翻译背景:天气信息可以支持登革热防备,尽管软件功能本身无法确定预测准确性。目标:本研究开发了一个基于网络的天气警报原型,集成了气象数据和 WhatsApp 通知,用于印度尼西亚巴鲁的登革热防备。方法:研究和开发遵循 2026 年 1 月 14 日至 2 月 4 日 ADDIE 模型的分析、设计和开发阶段。CodeIgniter 4 和 MySQL 通过 OpenWeather、数据存储、用户注册和自动通知支持基于坐标的天气检索。结果:原型覆盖8个区46个城中村。据报告,九个功能测试类别是成功的,包括网站可访问性、地理数据、天气集成、存储、通知、决策逻辑、注册、配置文件管理和界面响应能力。然而,详细的测试输入、执行计数和通知传送收据不可用。22天的开发周期在没有历史记录的情况下无法独立建立完整的30天降雨数据集。天气阈值仍然是基于文献的假设,没有当地的预测验证。结论:原型展示了初步的软件集成能力。在实施登革热防备操作之前,需要进行可重复的功能测试、降雨数据验证、治理保障措施和独立的流行病学验证。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在开发基于网络的登革热天气警报系统,以支持印度尼西亚巴鲁的登革热防备工作,因为天气信息可以支持登革热防备工作,尽管仅靠软件功能无法确定预测准确性。
设计与对象
该研究涉及开发一个集成气象数据和 WhatsApp 通知的基于网络的天气警报原型,覆盖印度尼西亚巴鲁八个区的 46 个城中村,时间为 2026 年 1 月 14 日至 2 月 4 日。
核心方法
该原型是使用 ADDIE 模型开发的,CodeIgniter 4 和 MySQL 通过 OpenWeather 支持基于坐标的天气检索、数据存储、用户注册和自动通知。
主要结果
据报告,九个功能测试类别是成功的,包括网站可访问性、地理数据、天气集成、存储、通知、决策逻辑、注册、配置文件管理和界面响应能力。
解释与创新
该原型展示了初步的软件集成功能,暗示了通过基于天气的警报来预防登革热的潜在工具。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,该原型可以通过整合天气数据和 WhatsApp 通知来支持登革热防备,可能有助于公共卫生干预的早期预警系统。
局限与证据强度
该研究缺乏详细的测试输入、执行计数和通知送达收据,并且22天的开发周期无法在没有历史记录的情况下独立建立完整的30天降雨数据集。证据是中等的。

Development and Functional Testing of a Web Based Dengue Weather Alert Prototype with WhatsApp Notifications in Palu, Indonesia

Authors: Iqbal Hidayat Syahril, Kurnia Hayat Hag, Diah Mutiarasari, Rizan Machmud
Original abstract (English)
Background: Weather information may support dengue preparedness, although software functionality alone cannot establish forecasting accuracy. Objectives: This study developed a web based weather alert prototype integrating meteorological data and WhatsApp notifications for dengue preparedness in Palu, Indonesia. Methods: Research and development followed the Analysis, Design, and Development phases of the ADDIE model from January 14 to February 4, 2026. CodeIgniter 4 and MySQL supported coordinate based weather retrieval through OpenWeather, data storage, user registration, and automated notifications. Results: The prototype covered 46 urban villages across eight districts. Nine functional testing categories were reported as successful, including website accessibility, geographic data, weather integration, storage, notifications, decision logic, registration, profile management, and interface responsiveness. However, detailed test inputs, execution counts, and notification delivery receipts were unavailable. The 22 day development period could not independently establish a complete 30 day rainfall dataset without historical records. Weather thresholds remained literature informed assumptions without local predictive validation. Conclusions: The prototype demonstrated preliminary software integration capabilities. Reproducible functional testing, rainfall data verification, governance safeguards, and independent epidemiological validation are required before operational implementation for dengue preparedness.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to develop a web-based dengue weather alert system to support dengue preparedness in Palu, Indonesia, as weather information may support dengue preparedness, although software functionality alone cannot establish forecasting accuracy.
Design and population
The study involved the development of a web-based weather alert prototype integrating meteorological data and WhatsApp notifications, covering 46 urban villages across eight districts in Palu, Indonesia, from January 14 to February 4, 2026.
Core methods
The prototype was developed using the ADDIE model, with CodeIgniter 4 and MySQL supporting coordinate-based weather retrieval through OpenWeather, data storage, user registration, and automated notifications.
Main results
Nine functional testing categories were reported as successful, including website accessibility, geographic data, weather integration, storage, notifications, decision logic, registration, profile management, and interface responsiveness.
Interpretation and novelty
The prototype demonstrated preliminary software integration capabilities, suggesting a potential tool for dengue preparedness through weather-based alerts.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that the prototype could support dengue preparedness by integrating weather data and WhatsApp notifications, potentially aiding in early warning systems for public health interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study lacks detailed test inputs, execution counts, and notification delivery receipts, and the 22-day development period could not independently establish a complete 30-day rainfall dataset without historical records. The evidence is moderate.

埃及伊蚊同时和相继同时感染 DENV-1、DENV-2 和 CHIKV 对 媒介 能力的影响

The consequence of simultaneous and sequential coinfections of Aedes aegypti with DENV-1, DENV-2, and CHIKV on vector competence
作者： do Nascimento R, Baptista A, da Silva A, Godoy R, Henrique-Gomes L, Campolina T, Nacif-Pimenta R, Pimenta P, Secundino N
摘要中文翻译在这项研究中,我们调查了寨卡病毒(媒介)与两种登革病毒血清型(DENV-1和DENV-2)同时感染对Ae. aegypti(BH株)的影响。总体而言,我们的研究结果表明,CHIKV–DENV共同感染在Ae. aegypti中表现出血清型依赖性结果。在感染后第7天(dpi),DENV-1的感染率和病毒载量显著低于单感染,而DENV-2在唾液中的病毒载量则有所增加。这些结果突显了在该共同宿主中,这种病毒共同感染的复杂性,并强调了在评估媒介能力及传播风险时,需要考虑不同的共同感染情况。关键词:Aedes aegypti,虫媒病毒共同感染,寨卡病毒,登革病毒血清型,顺序感染,同时感染,媒介能力。2.材料和方法2.1. 本研究中使用了Aedes aegypti(BH株),该蚊株由巴西Fiocruz-Minas的医学昆虫学实验室维持。摘要虫媒病毒引起的虫媒疾病(VBD)是热带地区的主要公共卫生威胁。根据世界卫生组织(WHO)的全球媒介控制响应,全球超过80%的人口生活在有VBD风险的地区。例如,Aedes aegypti是寨卡病毒(登革病毒)和登革病毒(DENV)的主要媒介,其在巴西的广泛分布促进了暴发的发生。在这些暴发期间,蚊虫可能获得单感染或共同感染,这些感染可能同时或顺序发生,可能影响传播动态。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查同时感染 CHIKV 和两种 DENV 血清型(DENV-1 和 DENV-2)对埃及伊蚊 媒介 能力的影响,弥补关于同时感染如何影响热带地区病毒 媒介 动态的知识差距。
设计与对象
该研究涉及 3 至 5 天大的雌性埃及伊蚊的实验感染,分为五组(每组 200 只)同时合并感染和四组(每组 300 只)顺序合并感染。蚊子在标准昆虫条件下饲养并通过膜饲感染。
核心方法
使用 BALB/c 小鼠的新鲜血液通过膜饲法感染蚊子。使用 RT-qPCR 进行病毒 RNA 定量,并收集唾液样本用于 传播 效率评估。统计分析包括 Kruskal-Wallis 检验和 Shapiro-Wilk 正态性检验。
主要结果
7 dpi时,与单一感染相比,CD1组的DENV-1感染率和病毒载量显着降低,而DENV-2则显示唾液中的病毒载量增加。在连续共感染的任何时间点均未检测到病毒载量的显着差异。与 DENV-1/CHIKV 合并感染组相比,单一感染组在 14 dpi 时的 CHIKV 感染率显着降低。
解释与创新
作者解释说,DENV-1和DENV-2在与CHIKV共同感染时表现出不同的传播动态,其中DENV-1显示感染率和病毒载量降低,而DENV-2显示唾液中病毒载量增加。这一发现凸显了蚊子中虫媒病毒之间复杂的相互作用。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可以为登革热和基孔肯雅热联合感染的 监测 策略和风险评估提供信息,因为它们证明了病毒相互作用如何影响埃及伊蚊的 监测 效率和 媒介 能力。
局限与证据强度
该研究进行了三个独立的生物学实验,并评估了实验间的变异性,但没有检测到显着差异,这可能会限制结果的可信度。由于受控的实验设置和统计测试的使用,证据是中等的。

The consequence of simultaneous and sequential coinfections of Aedes aegypti with DENV-1, DENV-2, and CHIKV on vector competence

Authors: do Nascimento R, Baptista A, da Silva A, Godoy R, Henrique-Gomes L, Campolina T, Nacif-Pimenta R, Pimenta P, Secundino N
Original abstract (English)
In this study, we investigated the effects of simultaneous coinfection with CHIKV and two DENV serotypes (DENV-1 and DENV-2) on the vector competence of Ae. Overall, our findings demonstrate that CHIKV–DENV coinfection in Ae. These results highlight the complexity of arboviral coinfections within this shared vector and emphasize the importance of considering different coinfection scenarios when evaluating vector competence and transmission risk in endemic settings.Keywords: Aedes aegypti, arbovirus coinfection, chikungunya virus, dengue virus serotypes, sequential infection, simultaneous infection, vector competence 2.Materials and methods2.1. aegypti (strain BH), maintained at the Laboratory of Medical Entomology, Fiocruz-Minas, Brazil, was used in this study. AbstractVector-borne diseases (VBD) caused by arboviruses represent a major public health threat in tropical regions. According to WHO’s Global Vector Control Response, more than 80% of the global population live in areas at risk for VBD. For instance, Aedes aegypti is the primary vector of dengue virus (DENV) and chikungunya virus (CHIKV), and its widespread distribution, particularly in Brazil, contributes to the occurrence of outbreaks. During these outbreaks, mosquitoes may acquire either monoinfections or coinfections, which can occur either simultaneously or sequentially, potentially influencing transmission dynamics. aegypti and described the characteristics of infection and transmission phenotypes following different orders of sequential arbovirus acquisition. Simultaneous coinfection resulted in serotype-dependent outcomes. At 7 days post-infection (dpi), DENV-1 infection rates and viral loads were significantly reduced compared with monoinfections, whereas DENV-2 showed increased viral loads in saliva....
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the effects of simultaneous coinfection with CHIKV and two DENV serotypes (DENV-1 and DENV-2) on the vector competence of Aedes aegypti mosquitoes, addressing the knowledge gap regarding how co-infections might influence viral transmission dynamics in tropical regions.
Design and population
The study involved experimental infections of three to five-day-old female Aedes aegypti mosquitoes divided into five groups (n = 200 per group) for simultaneous coinfection and four groups (n = 300 per group) for sequential coinfection. Mosquitoes were reared under standard insectary conditions and infected by membrane feeding.
Core methods
Mosquitoes were infected by membrane feeding using fresh blood from BALB/c mice. Viral RNA quantification was performed using RT-qPCR, and saliva samples were collected for transmission efficiency assessment. Statistical analyses included the Kruskal–Wallis test and Shapiro–Wilk test for normality.
Main results
At 7 dpi, DENV-1 infection rates and viral loads were significantly reduced in the CD1 group compared with monoinfections, while DENV-2 showed increased viral loads in saliva. No significant differences in viral loads were detected at any time point for sequential coinfections. CHIKV infection rates were significantly lower at 14 dpi in the monoinfected group compared to the DENV-1/CHIKV coinfection group.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that DENV-1 and DENV-2 exhibited different transmission dynamics when co-infected with CHIKV, with DENV-1 showing reduced infection rates and viral loads, and DENV-2 showing increased viral loads in saliva. This finding highlights the complex interactions between arboviruses in mosquitoes.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results can inform surveillance strategies and risk assessments for dengue and chikungunya co-infections, as they demonstrate how viral interactions may affect transmission efficiency and vector competence in Aedes aegypti.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study had three independent biological experiments, and inter-experiment variability was assessed, but no significant differences were detected, which may limit the confidence in the results. The evidence is moderate due to the controlled experimental setting and the use of statistical tests.

模拟厄尔尼诺-南方涛动对 2018 年至 2024 年阿根廷登革热 暴发 动态的影响

Modeling the impact of the El Niño–Southern Oscillation on the dynamics of dengue outbreaks in Argentina between 2018 and 2024
作者： Enet A, Lodigiani C, Arrúa Miano M, Lakkis G, Moreno Lell L, Panzarella C, Otero Legovich S, Bellizzi O, Bulló M, Perez-Lloret S
摘要中文翻译本研究评估了2018年至2024年阿根廷ENSO、当地气候变量和登革热暴发之间的关系。方法利用登革热病例和月平均气温、相对湿度和降雨量进行全国时空分析。对于每个部门和月份,我们使用了距离地面 2 米处的温度(以摄氏度,°C 为单位)和相对湿度(以 % 为单位)的每日值的月平均值,以及每月累计降雨量(以毫米,mm 为单位)。为了评估模型性能,我们根据拟合模型和空模型的偏差值,使用 Nagelkerke 方法计算了伪 R2。摘要背景登革热已成为全球流行病学关注的问题,2023-2024 年季节阿根廷报告了 531,617 例病例。厄尔尼诺-南方涛动(ENSO)现象与登革热动力学的变化有关。ENSO指标采用南方涛动指数(SOI)(厄尔尼诺相位≤-0.5,中性相位=-0.5至0.5,拉尼娜相位≥0.5)。我们使用集成嵌套拉普拉斯逼近 (INLA) 拟合分层贝叶斯模型。使用登革热病例的负二项分布来解释过度分散的情况。使用 Besag-York-Mollié 2 (BYM2) 结构对空间依赖性进行建模,并根据人口进行调整。结果最终模型的 Nagelkerke 伪 R2 为 0.537。后验概率和 95% 可信区间的暴露-反应曲线表明,在厄尔尼诺或中性阶段,当月平均气温在 20° 至 30° 摄氏度之间时,登革热风险增加,而在拉尼娜阶段,影响不太一致。湿度 >50% 与拉尼娜阶段登革热风险增加相关,陡度或绝对值较小...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在了解厄尔尼诺-南方涛动 (ENSO) 如何影响阿根廷的登革热 暴发,因为 登革热 已成为一个重要的流行病学问题,2023-2024 年季节报告了 531,617 例病例。
设计与对象
对 2018 年至 2024 年间阿根廷登革热病例分布进行了每月一次的分析,纳入了所有部门的气候参数和南方涛动指数 (SOI)。
核心方法
该研究使用了月平均温度和湿度数据以及累积降雨量,并应用了具有非线性效应和滞后的贝叶斯分层负二项式模型。模型选择基于 DIC 和 WAIC,并使用后验预测检查来评估模型的充分性。
主要结果
在厄尔尼诺现象或中性阶段,温度在 20° 至 30°C 之间时,登革热风险会增加,而在拉尼娜现象期间,湿度 >50% 与更高的风险相关。控制协变量后,负 SOI 评分与登革热风险增加相关。最终模型包括 SOI 与温度和湿度的相互作用,其中温度和湿度被建模为具有 2 个月滞后的非线性效应。
解释与创新
作者解释说,阿根廷的登革热 暴发 与温度、湿度和 SOI 异常独立相关,强调了 ENSO 指数在理解 暴发 动态方面的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,将 ENSO 指数纳入预测模型可以改善阿根廷登革热的早期预警系统和公共卫生干预措施。
局限与证据强度
该研究的证据是中等的,因为它依赖于观察数据并且没有建立因果关系。由于关注阿根廷并使用单个数据集,该模型的普遍性可能受到限制。

Modeling the impact of the El Niño–Southern Oscillation on the dynamics of dengue outbreaks in Argentina between 2018 and 2024

Authors: Enet A, Lodigiani C, Arrúa Miano M, Lakkis G, Moreno Lell L, Panzarella C, Otero Legovich S, Bellizzi O, Bulló M, Perez-Lloret S
Original abstract (English)
This study assessed the relationships among ENSO, local climatic variables, and dengue outbreaks in Argentina from 2018 to 2024.MethodsA nationwide spatiotemporal analysis was conducted using dengue cases and monthly mean temperature, relative humidity, and rainfall. For each department and month, we used the monthly mean of daily values for temperature (measured in degrees Celsius, °C) and relative humidity (measured in %), both at 2 meters above ground, as well as the monthly accumulated rainfall (measured in millimeters, mm). To assess model performance, we calculated a pseudo-R2 using Nagelkerke’s method, based on the deviance values from the fitted and null models. AbstractBackgroundDengue fever has become an epidemiological concern worldwide, with 531,617 cases reported in Argentina during the 2023–2024 season. The El Niño–Southern Oscillation (ENSO) phenomenon has been linked to changes in dengue dynamics. The Southern Oscillation Index (SOI) was used as the ENSO indicator (El Niño phase ≤ −0.5, neutral phase = −0.5 to 0.5, and La Niña phase ≥ 0.5). We fitted Hierarchical Bayesian models with Integrated Nested Laplace Approximation (INLA). A Negative Binomial distribution of dengue cases was used to account for overdispersion. Spatial dependence was modeled by using the Besag–York–Mollié 2 (BYM2) structure, adjusted for population.ResultsThe final model achieved a Nagelkerke pseudo-R2 of 0.537. Exposure-response curves of posterior probability and 95% credible intervals suggested that dengue risk increased when monthly mean temperatures were between 20° and 30° Celsius during the El Niño or neutral phase, with less consistent effects during the La Niña phase. Humidity >50% was associated with increased dengue risk during the La Niña phase, with less steep or abs...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to understand how the El Niño–Southern Oscillation (ENSO) influences dengue outbreaks in Argentina, as dengue fever has become a significant epidemiological concern with 531,617 cases reported during the 2023–2024 season.
Design and population
A nationwide analysis of dengue case distribution in Argentina between 2018 and 2024 was conducted at a monthly resolution, incorporating climate parameters and the Southern Oscillation Index (SOI) across all departments.
Core methods
The study used monthly mean temperature and humidity data, along with accumulated rainfall, and applied a Bayesian hierarchical negative binomial model with nonlinear effects and lags. Model selection was based on DIC and WAIC, and posterior predictive checks were used to assess model adequacy.
Main results
Dengue risk increased with temperatures between 20° and 30° Celsius during El Niño or neutral phases, while humidity >50% was linked to higher risk during La Niña. Negative SOI scores were associated with increased dengue risk after controlling for covariates. The final model included SOI interactions with temperature and humidity, with temperature and humidity modeled as nonlinear effects with a 2-month lag.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that dengue outbreaks in Argentina are independently associated with temperature, humidity, and SOI anomalies, highlighting the importance of ENSO indices in understanding outbreak dynamics.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that integrating ENSO indices into predictive models could improve early warning systems and public health interventions for dengue in Argentina.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence is moderate, as it relies on observational data and does not establish causality. The model's generalizability may be limited due to the focus on Argentina and the use of a single dataset.

7-O-磺化将木犀草素的靶标偏好从弗林蛋白酶重定向至 NS2B/NS3,并增强抗登革热活性

7-O-sulfonation redirects luteolin’s target preference from furin to NS2B/NS3 and enhances anti-dengue activity
作者： Chen H, Lao Z, Huo Y, Yu L, Zhou W, Li G
摘要中文翻译通过 qRT-PCR(测量病毒 RNA 丰度)和噬菌斑测定在 Vero 细胞中评估它们的抗 DENV 活性,并使用 CCK8 方法测定细胞毒性。本研究为进一步优化黄酮类药物 抗病毒药物 提供了结构框架。按照相应方法部分的规定,每次测定中均包括适当的载体(DMSO)和未经处理的对照。2.2细胞毒性测量细胞在96孔培养板中以6×103细胞/孔生长,并用浓度范围为0.39至200μM的化合物以两倍连续稀释进行处理。使用GAPDH作为看家基因,并使用2−ΔΔCT(循环阈值)方法进行相对定量。每个生物学重复包括三个技术重复。2.5蛋白酶抑制测定对于NS2B/NS3和弗林蛋白酶测定,根据初步剂量探索实验选择化合物浓度、底物浓度和孵育时间,以确保酶活性保持在线性范围内。使用与上述 NS2B/NS3 相同的非线性回归和 Lineweaver-Burk 分析方法确定动力学参数和抑制常数。背景登革热病毒(DENV)感染仍然是全球健康威胁,目前尚无批准用于临床的小分子 抗病毒药物。木犀草素是一种天然类黄酮,具有抗登革热活性,但溶解度差且效力中等。7-O位是代谢葡萄糖醛酸化的主要位点,而5-O位通过分子内氢键隔离,为衍生化提供了结构上不同的位置。方法合成了木犀草素的五种位点选择性衍生物(三个5-O-取代和两个7-O-取代)。对于机械...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在研究木犀草素的 7-O-磺化如何影响其靶点偏好和抗登革热活性,解决开发更有效的 抗病毒药物 药物对抗 抗病毒药物 的需求。
设计与对象
这项研究是在 Vero 细胞中进行的,这些细胞在 37°C、5% CO2 的 Dulbecco 改良 Eagle 培养基 (DMEM) 中生长和维持。每个生物复制包括三个技术复制,并且未处理的细胞被包括作为归一化的空白对照。
核心方法
使用 qRT-PCR 测量病毒 RNA 丰度和噬菌斑测定来评估抗 DENV 活性。使用CCK8方法测定细胞毒性。每次测定前将化合物溶解在二甲基亚砜 (DMSO) 中并在培养基中稀释,最终 DMSO 浓度调整至≤0.1% (v/v)。所有 抗病毒药物 测定的实验条件保持一致,以确保可比性。
主要结果
化合物 5 在 7-O 位具有 4-氟苯磺酰基,显示出最有效的抗 DENV 活性 (EC50 = 0.46 μM) 和最高的选择性指数 (SI = 28.5),这表明 抗病毒药物 的效力比木犀草素增加了 18 倍。在酶测定中,化合物 5 优先抑制病毒 NS2B/NS3 蛋白酶 (Ki = 11.54 μM),而不是 宿主 弗林蛋白酶 (Ki = 93 μM)。
解释与创新
作者解释说,7-O-磺化将木犀草素的靶标偏好从弗林蛋白酶重定向到 NS2B/NS3,从而增强了其抗登革热活性。这代表了一种修饰木犀草素以提高 抗病毒药物 功效的新方法。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,通过 7-O-磺化修饰木犀草素可能会产生更有效的 抗病毒药物 药物来对抗 登革病毒,这可能为未来的药物开发和 治疗 策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究使用Vero细胞作为模型,可能无法完全代表人类登革热感染。证据是中等的,因为结果是基于体外测定而不是体内或临床数据。

7-O-sulfonation redirects luteolin’s target preference from furin to NS2B/NS3 and enhances anti-dengue activity

Authors: Chen H, Lao Z, Huo Y, Yu L, Zhou W, Li G
Original abstract (English)
Their anti-DENV activity was evaluated in Vero cells by qRT-PCR (measuring viral RNA abundance) and plaque assays, and cytotoxicity was determined using the CCK8 method. This study provides a structural framework for the further optimization of flavonoid-based antiviral agents. Appropriate vehicle (DMSO) and untreated controls were included in each assay as specified in the respective method sections.2.2Cytotoxicity measurementCells were grown at 6 × 103 cells/well in 96-well culture plates and treated with compounds at concentrations ranging from 0.39 to 200 μM in twofold serial dilutions. GAPDH was used as the housekeeping gene and relative quantification was performed using the 2−ΔΔCT (cycle threshold) method. Each biological replicate included three technical replicates.2.5Protease inhibition assayFor both NS2B/NS3 and furin protease assays, the compound concentrations, substrate concentrations, and incubation times were selected based on preliminary dose-finding experiments to ensure that enzymatic activity remained within the linear range. Kinetic parameters and inhibition constants were determined using the same nonlinear regression and Lineweaver-Burk analysis methods as described above for NS2B/NS3. BackgroundDengue virus (DENV) infection remains a global health threat with no approved small-molecule antiviral for clinical use. Luteolin, a natural flavonoid, exhibits anti-dengue activity but suffers from poor solubility and moderate potency. The 7-O position is the primary site of metabolic glucuronidation, whereas the 5-O position is sequestered by intramolecular hydrogen bonding, offering structurally distinct positions for derivatization.MethodsFive site-selective derivatives of luteolin (three 5-O- and two 7-O-substituted) were synthesized. For mechanistic...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate how 7-O-sulfonation of luteolin affects its target preference and anti-dengue activity, addressing the need to develop more effective antiviral agents against dengue virus.
Design and population
The study was conducted in Vero cells, which were grown and maintained in Dulbecco’s modified Eagle’s medium (DMEM) at 37 °C in 5% CO2. Each biological replicate included three technical replicates, and untreated cells were included as blank controls for normalization.
Core methods
The anti-DENV activity was evaluated using qRT-PCR to measure viral RNA abundance and plaque assays. Cytotoxicity was determined using the CCK8 method. Compounds were dissolved in dimethyl sulfoxide (DMSO) and diluted in culture medium before each assay, with the final DMSO concentration adjusted to ≤0.1% (v/v). Experimental conditions were kept consistent across all antiviral assays to ensure comparability.
Main results
Compound 5, with a 4-fluorobenzenesulfonyl group at the 7-O position, showed the most potent anti-DENV activity (EC50 = 0.46 μM) and the highest selectivity index (SI = 28.5), representing an 18-fold increase in antiviral potency over luteolin. In enzymatic assays, compound 5 preferentially inhibited the viral NS2B/NS3 protease (Ki = 11.54 μM) over the host furin (Ki = 93 μM).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that 7-O-sulfonation redirected luteolin’s target preference from furin to NS2B/NS3, enhancing its anti-dengue activity. This represents a novel approach to modifying luteolin for improved antiviral efficacy.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that modifying luteolin through 7-O-sulfonation could lead to more effective antiviral agents against dengue virus, potentially informing future drug development and treatment strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study used Vero cells as a model, which may not fully represent human dengue infection. The evidence is moderate, as the results are based on in vitro assays and not in vivo or clinical data.

Molecular Detection of Arboviruses in Aedes spp. Larvae During Entomo-Virological Surveillance in a Hospital Area of Brasília, Federal District, Brazil

Authors: Pereira R, de Medeiros K, Reis M, de Oliveira D, de Oliveira M, Santos L, de Paula D, André A, Ramada M, de Oliveira W

This study investigated arboviruses in Aedes spp. Methods: Eighteen ovitraps were deployed for 26 weeks. Results: Dengue virus serotypes 1, 2, and 4, Zika virus, and Chikungunya virus were identified, with an overall positivity rate of 30.76%. Question and background
Arboviruses in hospital environments may represent a neglected risk of nosocomial infections.
Design and population
Eighteen ovitraps were deployed for 26 weeks to collect Aedes spp. larvae in a hospital area of Brasília, Federal District, Brazil.
Core methods
Larvae were pooled weekly and viral detection was performed by reverse transcription-quantitative PCR.
Main results
Dengue virus serotypes 1, 2, and 4, Zika virus, and Chikungunya virus were identified, with an overall positivity rate of 30.76%.
Interpretation and novelty
The surveillance strategy effectively detected arboviral RNA in a hospital setting, suggesting local arboviral circulation associated with the transmission triad.
Scientific and public-health significance
Entomo-virological surveillance followed by serological active survey of symptomatic individuals is helpful to identify hotspots of early arbovirus transmission.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations.

One Health 监测、预警和响应的跨学科以人为本的架构框架:利用人工智能建模和物联网环境监测

Interdisciplinary human-centric architectural framework for One Health surveillance, early warning, and response: leveraging AI modelling and IoT environmental monitoring
作者： Alzahrani A, Alshammari H, Kostkova P
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在利用人工智能建模和物联网环境监测,为 One Health 监测、早期预警和响应开发一个以人为中心的跨学科架构框架。这在同一个卫生系统中尤为重要,在该系统中,不公平的数据收集是世界资源匮乏地区缺乏资源和获取途径的必然结果。
设计与对象
The study involved a real-world pilot for mosquito-borne disease surveillance deployed with users in Brazil and Madeira over 2 years. These studies demonstrated that misinformation often originates from a small number of accounts and sources.
核心方法
还建议过滤低质量、有偏见的新闻和错误信息的方法,如对 COVID-19 期间 Twitter 和 HPV 疫苗 错误信息的分析所示。该架构由实现数据管道的六层组成,包括同一个健康数据源、通过联合架构进行的数据集成、用于早期预警的时空和人工智能建模,以及供决策者使用的基于组件的可配置仪表板。
主要结果
输入性登革热病例从2023年的631例增加到2024年的904例,同时基孔肯雅热和疟疾病例也有所增加。分析人类、动物和废水等环境来源的基因组数据的人工智能模型已经证明了早期预警和加强抗菌药物管理 (AMS) 的潜力。
解释与创新
这些实际部署的架构和结果旨在说明 OHSEW 框架的可行性,并概述了进一步研究的方向。One Health 是检测现有传染病 暴发 和潜在 跨物种溢出(跨物种 暴发)以及识别人际 暴发 环境(下一次大流行最有可能爆发)的驱动方法。
科研与公卫意义
针对人类疾病 监测(例如流感或流感样疾病 (ILI))实施 OHSEW 将需要公共卫生机构的参与、访问和共享报告的数据以及使用 InfluenzaNet2 等参与性 监测 应用程序提供的数据。
局限与证据强度
通用性 虽然试点是针对蚊媒疾病 监测 实施的,但该框架和相关工具可推广到其他 监测 的常规数据收集中——我们目前正在探索针对蜱虫 监测 的数字工具的适应性。提供的证据不报告具体的数字结果或统计分析。

Interdisciplinary human-centric architectural framework for One Health surveillance, early warning, and response: leveraging AI modelling and IoT environmental monitoring

Authors: Alzahrani A, Alshammari H, Kostkova P
Original abstract (English)
The architectures and results of these real-world deployments are presented to illustrate the feasibility of the OHSEW framework, with directions for further research outlined.Keywords: AI modelling, architecture framework, Internet of Things, One Health, vector-borne disease 4.Discussion and Future WorkIn this section, we will discuss the strengths of the framework to consider when implementing OHSEW, the key challenges to address, and opportunities for scale-up.4.1.StrengthsThe main strengths are interdisciplinarity, privacy, transparency, data standardisation and interoperability, and generalisability. EHDS establishes common standards for health data quality, interoperability, and cross-border access, and, as a result, should enable AI model training on diverse data from European populations, improving fairness. AbstractSuccessful pandemic preparedness and sustainable healthcare security increasingly rely on One Health—linking humans, animals, and the environment. Novel real-time geolocated data sources from social media, the web, digital traces, and Internet of Things (IoT) sensors, combined with traditional surveillance data, are increasingly used to power AI models and enhance environmental sensing that is essential for preparedness and response in the One Health context. Despite the abundance of data streams and sensing data, the potential of AI and IoT environmental monitoring for One Health remains unfulfilled. The OHSEW...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop an interdisciplinary human-centric architectural framework for One Health surveillance, early warning, and response, leveraging AI modelling and IoT environmental monitoring. This is of particular importance in One Health systems, where inequitable data collection is an inevitable outcome of the lack of resources and access in less-resourced parts of the world.
Design and population
The study involved a real-world pilot for mosquito-borne disease surveillance deployed with users in Brazil and Madeira over 2 years. These studies demonstrated that misinformation often originates from a small number of accounts and sources.
Core methods
Methods for filtering low-quality, biased news and misinformation are also recommended, as shown by an analysis of Twitter during COVID-19 and around HPV vaccine misinformation. The architecture consists of six layers implementing the data pipeline, including One Health Data Sources, Data Integration through a Federated Architecture, Spatiotemporal and AI Modelling for Early Warning, and Component-based Configurable Dashboards for decision-makers.
Main results
Imported dengue cases increased from 631 in 2023 to 904 in 2024, while chikungunya and malaria cases also increased. AI models analysing genomic data from humans, animals, and environmental sources, such as wastewater, have demonstrated the potential for early warning and strengthening antimicrobial stewardship (AMS).
Interpretation and novelty
The architectures and results of these real-world deployments are presented to illustrate the feasibility of the OHSEW framework, with directions for further research outlined. One Health is the driving approach for detecting outbreaks of existing communicable diseases and potential spillovers (cross-species outbreaks) and for identifying settings of human-to-human transmission–the most likely onset of the next pandemic.
Scientific and public-health significance
Implementing OHSEW for human disease surveillance, such as influenza or influenza-like illness (ILI), will require engagement of public health agencies, access to and sharing of reported data, and use of data provided by participatory surveillance apps such as InfluenzaNet2.
Limitations and evidence strength
Generalisability While the pilot was implemented for mosquito-borne disease surveillance, the framework and associated tools are generalisable to routine data collection for other vectors—we are currently exploring the adaptation of the digital tools for tick surveillance. The supplied evidence does not report specific numerical findings or statistical analyses.

报告中登革热病例的时间模式(2020-2025年)以及沙特阿拉伯朱宰恩地区初级卫生保健中心就诊者对登革热相关知识、态度和行为的分析

TEMPORAL PATTERNS IN REPORTED DENGUE CASES (2020-2025) AND DENGUE-RELATED KNOWLEDGE, ATTITUDES, AND PRACTICES AMONG PRIMARY HEALTHCARE CENTER ATTENDEES IN JAZAN, SAUDI ARABIA
作者： Authors unavailable
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查沙特阿拉伯朱宰恩地区初级卫生保健中心就诊者中登革热病例报告的时间模式及其相关知识、态度和行为,以应对该地区登革热的公共卫生问题。
设计与对象
本研究结合了登革热病例的回顾性监测和沙特阿拉伯朱宰恩地区初级卫生保健中心就诊者中进行的横断面知识、态度和行为(KAP)评估。研究纳入了430名完成结构化KAP问卷的成年人。
核心方法
病例数量按年份汇总并进行比较;KAP数据通过描述性统计、非参数检验、斯皮尔曼相关性、可靠性分析和多元线性回归进行分析。
主要结果
The number of laboratory-confirmed dengue cases varied significantly across the study period, with 33 in 2020, 24 in 2021, 177 in 2023, 452 in 2024, and 32 in 2025. Among PHC attendees, mean knowledge, attitude, practice, and total KAP scores were 83.69, 75.40, 87.55, and 82.21, respectively, with 68.8%, 54.2%, 79.8%, and 79.1% meeting the 75% threshold.
解释与创新
研究结果突显了登革热病例波动的特性,并强调了提高社区意识和参与的必要性,以及人口学因素与KAP结果之间的关联。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持在沙特阿拉伯朱宰恩地区持续监测和针对性的媒介知识普及项目的重要性,以增强社区预防措施并减少登革热传播。
局限与证据强度
由于缺乏分母,本研究未能获得年度基于人口的发病率率,且KAP调查仅限于PHC就诊者,可能影响结果的普遍适用性。由于这些限制,证据强度中等。

TEMPORAL PATTERNS IN REPORTED DENGUE CASES (2020-2025) AND DENGUE-RELATED KNOWLEDGE, ATTITUDES, AND PRACTICES AMONG PRIMARY HEALTHCARE CENTER ATTENDEES IN JAZAN, SAUDI ARABIA

Authors: Authors unavailable
Original abstract (English)
This study used the combination of retrospective surveillance and cross-sectional Knowledge, Attitude and Practices (KAP) assessment in Jazan Region. Confirmed cases varied markedly across the study period: 33 in 2020, 24 in 2021, 177 in 2022, 270 in 2023, 452 in 2024, and 32 in 2025. Among PHC visitors, mean standardized knowledge, attitude, practice, and total KAP scores were 83.69, 75.40, 87.55, and 82.21, respectively; 68.8%, 54.2%, 79.8%, and 79.1% met the study-defined 75% thresholds. The results endorse continued monitoring and targeted PHC-based vector literacy and community-engagement efforts. TEMPORAL PATTERNS IN REPORTED DENGUE CASES (2020-2025) AND DENGUE-RELATED KNOWLEDGE, ATTITUDES, AND PRACTICES AMONG PRIMARY HEALTHCARE CENTER ATTENDEES IN JAZAN, SAUDI ARABIA Abstract Dengue remains a major public-health concern in southwestern Saudi Arabia, where local surveillance and community prevention are both essential. The number of laboratory confirmed dengue cases reported via the Saudi Ministry of Health HESN Plus system from 2020 until 2025 was included in Component A. There were 430 adult primary healthcare (PHC) visitors who answered the struct...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate temporal patterns in reported dengue cases and dengue-related knowledge, attitudes, and practices among primary healthcare center attendees in Jazan, Saudi Arabia, to address the public-health concern of dengue in the region.
Design and population
The study combined retrospective surveillance of dengue cases and a cross-sectional Knowledge, Attitude, and Practices (KAP) assessment conducted among primary healthcare center attendees in Jazan, Saudi Arabia. It included 430 adults who completed a structured KAP questionnaire.
Core methods
Case counts were aggregated and compared across years; KAP data were analyzed using descriptive statistics, nonparametric tests, Spearman correlations, reliability analysis, and multiple linear regression.
Main results
The number of laboratory-confirmed dengue cases varied significantly across the study period, with 33 in 2020, 24 in 2021, 177 in 2023, 452 in 2024, and 32 in 2025. Among PHC attendees, mean knowledge, attitude, practice, and total KAP scores were 83.69, 75.40, 87.55, and 82.21, respectively, with 68.8%, 54.2%, 79.8%, and 79.1% meeting the 75% threshold.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the fluctuating nature of dengue cases and the need for improved community awareness and engagement, as well as the association between demographic factors and KAP outcomes.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results support the importance of continued monitoring and targeted vector literacy programs in Jazan, Saudi Arabia, to enhance community prevention efforts and reduce dengue transmission.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study lacked annual population-based incidence rates due to the absence of denominators, and the KAP survey was limited to PHC attendees, potentially affecting generalizability. The evidence is moderate due to these limitations.

Journal: Reviews in medical virology  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-01  |  DOI: 10.1002/rmv.70216  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Future scheduled publication  |  Citation: 2026, 36, 6, e70216
A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

墨西哥2020-2024年登革热的区域与时间动态:一项全面的流行病学分析。

Regional and Temporal Dynamics of Dengue in Mexico (2020-2024): A Comprehensive Epidemiological Analysis.
作者： Blanca Taboada, Selene Zárate, Lorena Díaz-González, Juan Manuel Hurtado
摘要中文翻译登革热仍然是墨西哥的主要公共卫生问题,近年来其流行程度和临床负担显著增加。本研究利用2020年至2024年的全国登革热数据,全面概述了登革热流行病学景观的变化,重点分析了地理分布、时间趋势、血清型动态以及疾病严重程度。在这一时期,墨西哥的登革热病例数几乎增加了五倍,2024年达到超过123,000例确诊病例和426例死亡,这是有记录以来最高的年度病例数。季节性模式发生了变化,持续的登革热传播从2023年底延伸到了不寻常的冬季月份。血清型数据揭示了主要病毒血清型的变化,DENV2在2022年之前占主导地位,DENV1在2021年达到高峰,而DENV3则从2022年开始急剧上升,成为2023年的主导血清型,并在2024年几乎成为唯一流行的血清型。这种变化与登革热病例数的持续上升以及警告信号的增加(从2020年的16.9%上升到2023-2024年的>42%)相吻合,同时医院入院率也显著提高,而重症登革热病例仍低于5%。尽管DENV3在全国范围内占主导地位,但DENV1和DENV2在某些州仍持续存在,而DENV4则在南部和北部地区偶有流行。一些传统上登革热流行率较低的州出现了非典型的流行模式,突显了登革热暴发可能突然扩展的潜在风险。气候分析显示,温度与DENV3的流行率之间存在一致的正相关关系,而降水的影响则因年份而异。比值比分析表明,不同血清型在不同年份的严重程度存在差异。这些发现突显了墨西哥登革热传播模式的异质性和动态性,并强调了制定区域性监测、预防和临床准备策略的必要性。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述聚焦于2020年至2024年墨西哥登革热的区域与时间动态,分析了地理分布、时间趋势、血清型动态以及疾病严重程度。核心问题是了解这一时期登革热传播模式的变化及其对公共卫生的影响。
证据基础与方法
该评价利用了2020年至2024年的国家监测数据。它包括对血清型特异性数据、气候因素和住院率的综合分析。研究设计基于观察流行病学数据,没有明确提及荟萃分析或共识过程。
共识与结论
墨西哥的登革热 重症登革热 和临床负担大幅增加,到 2024 年 DENV3 将成为主导。审查还指出,重症登革热 病例的比例仍低于 5%,而警告信号显着增加。研究结果强调了登革热 传播 在不同地区和年份的动态和异质性。
争议与缺口
审查发现,随着时间的推移,主要血清型发生了变化,从 2022 年起,DENV3 急剧上升。然而,驱动这种转变的确切机制尚未得到充分解释。此外,研究指出,一些传统上低 患病率 的状态经历了非典型的 暴发,但这些变化的原因尚未明确说明。
科研与实践启示
该综述强调,由于流行病学形势的变化,需要制定区域 监测、预防和临床准备策略。它还表明需要进一步的研究来了解导致血清型优势转变的因素以及某些州非典型 暴发 背后的原因。

Regional and Temporal Dynamics of Dengue in Mexico (2020-2024): A Comprehensive Epidemiological Analysis.

Authors: Blanca Taboada, Selene Zárate, Lorena Díaz-González, Juan Manuel Hurtado
Original abstract (English)
Dengue remains a major public health concern in Mexico, with incidence and clinical burden increasing substantially in recent years. Using national surveillance data from 2020 to 2024, this study provides a comprehensive overview of the changing epidemiological landscape, with an emphasis on geographic distribution, temporal trends, serotype dynamics, and disease severity. Over this period, Mexico saw an almost fivefold increase in dengue cases, reaching over 123,000 confirmed infections and 426 deaths in 2024, the highest annual number recorded. Seasonal patterns shifted, with sustained transmission extending into unusual winter months from late 2023. Serotyping data revealed a shift in predominant virus serotypes, with DENV2 predominating until 2022, DENV1 peaking in 2021, and DENV3 rising sharply from 2022 onward, becoming dominant in 2023 and almost exclusive by 2024. This shift coincided with a sustained rise in dengue with warning signs (from 16.9% in 2020 to > 42% in 2023-2024) and higher hospitalisation rates, while severe dengue remained below 5%. Although DENV3 dominated nationally, DENV1 and DENV2 persisted in specific states, and DENV4 circulated sporadically, particularly in southern and northern regions. Several traditionally low-prevalence states experienced atypical outbreaks, underscoring the potential for sudden epidemic expansion. Climatic analysis showed a consistent positive association between temperature and DENV3 prevalence, with precipitation effects varying by year. Odds ratio analysis suggested serotype-specific differences in severity across years. These findings underscore the heterogeneous and dynamic nature of dengue transmission in Mexico and highlight the need for regional surveillance, prevention, and clinical preparedness strategies.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review focuses on the regional and temporal dynamics of dengue in Mexico from 2020 to 2024, examining geographic distribution, temporal trends, serotype dynamics, and disease severity. The central question is to understand how dengue transmission patterns have evolved over this period and their implications for public health.
Evidence base and method
The review utilizes national surveillance data from 2020 to 2024. It includes a comprehensive analysis of serotype-specific data, climatic factors, and hospitalization rates. The study design is based on observational epidemiological data, with no explicit mention of meta-analysis or consensus processes.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that dengue incidence and clinical burden have increased substantially in Mexico, with DENV3 becoming dominant by 2024. It also notes that severe dengue remained below 5% of cases, while warning signs increased significantly. The findings highlight the dynamic and heterogeneous nature of dengue transmission across different regions and years.
Controversies and gaps
The review identifies a shift in predominant serotypes over time, with DENV3 rising sharply from 2022 onward. However, the exact mechanisms driving this shift are not fully explained. Additionally, the study notes that several traditionally low-prevalence states experienced atypical outbreaks, but the reasons for these variations are not clearly addressed.
Research and practice implications
The review emphasizes the need for regional surveillance, prevention, and clinical preparedness strategies due to the changing epidemiological landscape. It also suggests that further research is required to understand the factors contributing to the shift in serotype dominance and the reasons behind the atypical outbreaks in certain states.

New Perspectives in Prevention, Control, and Treatment of Dengue Fever
作者： Jairo Castellar-Lopez, Yeini Julieth Gutiérrez Rodríguez, Gabriela Alejandra Cáceres, Monica Gomez Pulido, Gabriela Lara Barreto, Noemi Ramirez, Santiago Alcazar Santos, Marian Mares Blancas, Diego Castro Garcia, Joseph León Insignares

范围与问题
登革热是一种蚊媒病毒性疾病,主要由埃及伊蚊传播,在全球热带和亚热带地区流行。

New Perspectives in Prevention, Control, and Treatment of Dengue Fever

Authors: Jairo Castellar-Lopez, Yeini Julieth Gutiérrez Rodríguez, Gabriela Alejandra Cáceres, Monica Gomez Pulido, Gabriela Lara Barreto, Noemi Ramirez, Santiago Alcazar Santos, Marian Mares Blancas, Diego Castro Garcia, Joseph León Insignares
Original abstract (English)
Dengue is a mosquito-borne viral disease primarily transmitted by Aedes aegypti and is endemic in tropical and subtropical regions worldwide. It represents a major global public health challenge because of its rapid expansion and increasing incidence. Clinical manifestations range from mild febrile illness to severe disease with hemorrhage and death. No specific antiviral treatment exists, and prevention has relied on vector control to reduce human–mosquito contact. Although vaccines are available, access remains limited. In the Americas, dengue transmission has intensified markedly in recent years. The largest epidemic occurred in 2024, with more than 12.6 million cases and over 7700 deaths. In 2025, reports showed 4 million suspected cases, including 8966 severe cases and 2207 deaths. This persistent burden may be driven by climate variability, urbanization, mobility, environmental change, and widespread vector distribution, all of which contribute to ongoing transmission and disease severity. These trends underscore the critical need to strengthen surveillance, improve integrated vector management, expand vaccine access, and develop effective therapies to reduce the health, social, and economic impact of this disease. Accordingly, effective preventive and therapeutic strategies are needed to address this persistent and growing threat.

Scope and question
Dengue is a mosquito-borne viral disease primarily transmitted by Aedes aegypti and is endemic in tropical and subtropical regions worldwide.
Evidence base and method
It represents a major global public health challenge because of its rapid expansion and increasing incidence. These trends underscore the critical need to strengthen surveillance, improve integrated vector management, expand vaccine access, and develop effective therapies to reduce the health, social, and economic impact of this disease.
Consensus and conclusions
Clinical manifestations range from mild febrile illness to severe disease with hemorrhage and death. Although vaccines are available, access remains limited.
Controversies and gaps
In the Americas, dengue transmission has intensified markedly in recent years. The largest epidemic occurred in 2024, with more than 12.6 million cases and over 7700 deaths.

In 2025, reports showed 4 million suspected cases, including 8966 severe cases and 2207 deaths. This persistent burden may be driven by climate variability, urbanization, mobility, environmental change, and widespread vector distribution, all of which contribute to ongoing transmission and disease severity.

温度、降雨量和湿度在城市环境中驾驶 登革病毒 传播 的作用:系统文献综述

The Role of Temperature, Rainfall, and Humidity in Driving Dengue Virus Transmission in Urban Environments: A Systematic Literature Review
作者： LUQMAN HAKIM ADI FIZNI
摘要中文翻译这项系统文献综述探讨了气候驱动的登革热模型中的关键局限性,这些模型目前依赖于宏观层面的气候或社会经济指标——例如州级的人类发展指数(HDI)得分——而未能考虑城市内部的微气候、城市热岛(UHI)效应以及局部人类活动。此外,城市气候适应措施,如人工绿色基础设施,可能会无意中创造热避难所并为入侵性登革热媒介如埃及伊蚊(Aedes albopictus)和按蚊(Anopheles stephensi)提供繁殖场所,而被动公共卫生措施通常忽略无症状疾病的传播。为弥合这些跨学科的差距,本研究旨在评估城市内部微气候和生态驱动因素的精细尺度,综合高分辨率时空和机器学习预测性预报模型,并制定一个跨部门的城市健康准备模型,将城市规划与登革热控制相结合。本综述聚焦于印度城市环境中的人群,评估了2017年至2026年间发表的同行评审期刊文章。文献检索使用查询字符串 TITLE-ABS-KEY (("urban population") AND ("climate" OR "temperature" OR "rain" OR "humidity" OR "weather") AND ("dengue" OR "登革热")) AND PUBYEAR > 2016 AND PUBYEAR < 2027。综述特别包括前瞻性观察性研究和高分辨率时空建模研究,这些研究评估了局部环境或地表温度、相对湿度、降雨量、绿色基础设施以及次邻里范围内的疾病传播。排除了评估旅行输入病例、实验室控制的蚊虫(缺乏人类聚居地数据)、空间聚合的宏观层面代理变量、孤立的非热气候变量、仅定性设计或未经验证的软件和海报文档的研究。最终,本综述综合了证据,说明局部微气候变化和绿色基础设施如何影响登革热的生态和传播。通过整合实时血清学数据、微环境数据、登革热指数以及集成预测技术,该框架超越了被动公共卫生措施,为市政城市规划者和公共卫生官员提供可操作的证据
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
这篇系统文献综述解决了气候驱动的 媒介 疾病模型的关键局限性,该模型目前依赖于宏观层面的气候或社会经济指标(例如州级人类发展指数得分),并且未能考虑城市内的微气候、城市热岛 (UHI) 效应和局部人口流动。
证据基础与方法
该综述特别包括前瞻性观测 队列研究 研究和高分辨率时空建模研究,用于评估局部环境或表面温度、相对湿度、降雨量、绿色基础设施和子社区疾病 发病率。评估旅行输入病例、没有人类住区数据的实验室控制蚊子、空间聚合的宏观代理、孤立的非热气候变量、纯定性设计或未经验证的软件和海报文档的研究被排除在外。
共识与结论
该综述综合了局部微气候变化和绿色基础设施如何影响 媒介 生态和 登革病毒 传播 的证据。它强调需要将微环境数据集成到预测模型中,以更好地理解和管理城市环境中的 登革病毒 传播。
争议与缺口
城市气候适应措施(例如工程绿色基础设施)可能会无意中为白纹伊蚊和斯氏按蚊等入侵性 媒介 创造热避难所和繁殖地。被动公共卫生监测通常会忽略无症状疾病传播,这可能会影响当前模型的准确性。
科研与实践启示
通过整合实时血清学 监测、微环境数据、媒介 指数和集合预测技术,该框架超越了被动的 监测,为城市规划者和公共卫生官员提供了可操作的证据。

The Role of Temperature, Rainfall, and Humidity in Driving Dengue Virus Transmission in Urban Environments: A Systematic Literature Review

Authors: LUQMAN HAKIM ADI FIZNI
Original abstract (English)
This systematic literature review addresses critical limitations in climate-driven vector disease models, which currently rely on macro-level climatic or socioeconomic indicators—such as state-level Human Development Index scores—and fail to account for intra-urban microclimates, Urban Heat Island (UHI) effects, and localized human mobility. Furthermore, urban climate adaptation measures like engineered green infrastructure can inadvertently create thermal refuges and breeding sites for invasive vectors such as Aedes albopictus and Anopheles stephensi, while passive public health surveillance routinely omits asymptomatic disease transmission. To bridge these interdisciplinary gaps, the study aims to evaluate fine-scale intra-urban microclimatic and ecological drivers, synthesize high-resolution spatial-temporal and machine learning predictive forecasting models, and formulate a cross-sectoral urban health preparedness model that aligns urban planning with vector control. Focusing on human populations within urban environments in India, the review evaluates peer-reviewed journal articles published between 2017 and 2026. Literature is identified using the query string TITLE-ABS-KEY (("urban population") AND ("climate" OR "temperature" OR "rain" OR "humidity" OR "weather") AND ("dengue" OR "dengue virus")) AND PUBYEAR > 2016 AND PUBYEAR < 2027. The review specifically includes prospective observational cohort studies and high-resolution spatial-temporal modeling research that evaluate localized ambient or surface temperature, relative humidity, rainfall, green infrastructure, and sub-neighborhood disease incidence. Studies evaluating travel-imported cases, laboratory-controlled mosquitoes without human settlement data, spatially aggregated macro-level proxies, non-thermal climate variables in isolation, qualitative-only designs, or unvalidated software and poster documentation are excluded. Ultimately, this review synthesizes evidence on how localized microclimatic variations and green infrastructure influence vector ecology and dengue virus transmission. By integrating real-time serological surveillance, micro-environmental data, vector indices, and ensemble forecasting techniques, the framework advances beyond passive surveillance to provide actionable evidence for municipal urban planners and public health officials
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This systematic literature review addresses critical limitations in climate-driven vector disease models, which currently rely on macro-level climatic or socioeconomic indicators—such as state-level Human Development Index scores—and fail to account for intra-urban microclimates, Urban Heat Island (UHI) effects, and localized human mobility.
Evidence base and method
The review specifically includes prospective observational cohort studies and high-resolution spatial-temporal modeling research that evaluate localized ambient or surface temperature, relative humidity, rainfall, green infrastructure, and sub-neighborhood disease incidence. Studies evaluating travel-imported cases, laboratory-controlled mosquitoes without human settlement data, spatially aggregated macro-level proxies, non-thermal climate variables in isolation, qualitative-only designs, or unvalidated software and poster documentation are excluded.
Consensus and conclusions
The review synthesizes evidence on how localized microclimatic variations and green infrastructure influence vector ecology and dengue virus transmission. It highlights the need for integrating micro-environmental data into predictive models to better understand and manage dengue virus transmission in urban settings.
Controversies and gaps
Urban climate adaptation measures like engineered green infrastructure can inadvertently create thermal refuges and breeding sites for invasive vectors such as Aedes albopictus and Anopheles stephensi. Passive public health surveillance routinely omits asymptomatic disease transmission, which may affect the accuracy of current models.
Research and practice implications
By integrating real-time serological surveillance, micro-environmental data, vector indices, and ensemble forecasting techniques, the framework advances beyond passive surveillance to provide actionable evidence for municipal urban planners and public health officials.

纳米技术 疫苗 用于治疗疟疾、结核病、艾滋病毒和登革热:全球南方的转化证据和实施

Nanotechnology-Enabled Vaccines for Malaria, Tuberculosis, HIV, and Dengue: Translational Evidence and Implementation in the Global South
作者： Abdelrahman Aly Mohamed, Ahmed Khairy, Mina Nageh, Mohamed Fawzi Hemida, Marium Aisha, Mahdi Ahmed, Lindsey Dixon, Yossef S. Alnasser
摘要中文翻译纳米级材料可以支持 疫苗 递送、抗原展示和免疫刺激,但成功的平台功能或增强的免疫原性并不一定能建立有意义或持久的临床保护。这篇叙述性综述审查了 2016 年 1 月至 2026 年 4 月发表的证据,以及选定的基础研究,用于纳米技术支持的 疫苗 对抗疟疾、结核病、人类免疫缺陷病毒 (HIV) 和登革热,重点关注它们与全球南方的相关性。选择这些疾病是因为其巨大的公共卫生负担、疫苗接种 的独特生物障碍以及转化发展的对比阶段,从而能够在临床前研究、人类免疫原性、临床疗效和实施证据之间进行比较。该合成将纳米级载体、抗原展示支架和佐剂的功能贡献与完整 疫苗 制剂的性能区分开来。Malaria 在已审查的平台中拥有最先进的临床和实施证据。结核病在确定的感染成年人群中具有疾病预防证据,而艾滋病毒和登革热平台仍主要处于临床前或早期临床阶段。纳米制剂的临床功效本身并不能量化归因于其纳米级成分的增量益处。在疾病中,转化价值取决于抗原的适用性、疾病相关的免疫功能、耐久性、制剂特异性的安全性以及对目标人群的适用性。来自选定试验 队列研究 的证据不能自动推广到异构的全球南方环境中。因此,必须与生物性能一起评估生产准备情况、储存要求、负担得起的完整时间表、监管能力和公平获取。该审查确定了平台功能、临床效益和可行实施之间的联系在哪些方面得到了支持,哪些方面尚未建立。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述重点关注纳米技术支持的 疫苗 for 登革病毒,特别检查其在全球南方的转化证据和实施潜力。核心问题是,考虑到与该疾病相关的独特的生物学和公共卫生挑战,这些 疫苗 是否能够提供有意义且持久的针对登革热的临床保护。
证据基础与方法
该评论是对 2016 年 1 月至 2026 年 4 月期间发表的证据的叙述性综合,其中包括选定的基础研究。它不报告荟萃分析或汇总估计,而是提供证据的定性概述,强调 疫苗 制剂中纳米级载体、抗原展示支架和佐剂的功能贡献。
共识与结论
登革热在审查的平台中拥有最先进的临床和实施证据。它强调,纳米制剂的临床功效不会自动量化其纳米级成分的增量益处,并且转化价值取决于抗原适用性、免疫功能、耐久性和制剂特定安全性等因素。
争议与缺口
有证据表明,完整的 疫苗 配方的性能不能仅仅归因于其纳米级成分。此外,审查指出,由于异质性,选定试验 队列研究 的证据可能无法推广到所有南半球国家。该审查还确定了在理解平台功能、临床效益和可行实施之间的联系方面的差距。
科研与实践启示
该审查强调需要评估生产准备情况、存储要求、负担得起的完整时间表、监管能力以及公平获取和生物性能。它还表明需要进一步研究来确定 疫苗 配方中纳米级成分的增量效益,并提高全球南方不同人群的普遍适用性。

Nanotechnology-Enabled Vaccines for Malaria, Tuberculosis, HIV, and Dengue: Translational Evidence and Implementation in the Global South

Authors: Abdelrahman Aly Mohamed, Ahmed Khairy, Mina Nageh, Mohamed Fawzi Hemida, Marium Aisha, Mahdi Ahmed, Lindsey Dixon, Yossef S. Alnasser
Original abstract (English)
Nanoscale materials can support vaccine delivery, antigen display and immune stimulation, but successful platform function or enhanced immunogenicity does not necessarily establish meaningful or durable clinical protection. This narrative review examines evidence published from January 2016 through April 2026, with selected foundational studies, for nanotechnology-enabled vaccines against malaria, tuberculosis, human immunodeficiency virus (HIV) and dengue, focusing on their relevance to the Global South. These diseases were selected for their substantial public-health burden, distinct biological barriers to vaccination and contrasting stages of translational development, enabling comparison across preclinical research, human immunogenicity, clinical efficacy and implementation evidence. The synthesis distinguishes the functional contribution of nanoscale carriers, antigen-display scaffolds and adjuvants from the performance of complete vaccine formulations. Malaria has the most advanced clinical and implementation evidence among the reviewed platforms. Tuberculosis has disease-prevention evidence in a defined infected adult population, and HIV and dengue platforms remain predominantly at preclinical or early clinical stages. Clinical efficacy of a nano-enabled formulation does not, by itself, quantify the incremental benefit attributable to its nanoscale component. Across diseases, translational value depends on antigen suitability, disease-relevant immune function, durability, formulation-specific safety and applicability to the intended population. Evidence from selected trial cohorts cannot automatically be generalized across heterogeneous Global South settings. Manufacturing readiness, storage requirements, affordable complete schedules, regulatory capacity and equitable access must therefore be assessed alongside biological performance. The review identifies where links between platform function, clinical benefit and feasible implementation are supported and where they remain unestablished.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review focuses on nanotechnology-enabled vaccines for dengue virus, specifically examining their translational evidence and implementation potential in the Global South. The central question is whether these vaccines can provide meaningful and durable clinical protection against dengue, considering the unique biological and public health challenges associated with the disease.
Evidence base and method
The review is a narrative synthesis of evidence published from January 2016 through April 2026, with selected foundational studies. It does not report on meta-analysis or pooled estimates but instead provides a qualitative overview of the evidence, emphasizing the functional contributions of nanoscale carriers, antigen-display scaffolds, and adjuvants in vaccine formulations.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that dengue has the most advanced clinical and implementation evidence among the reviewed platforms. It highlights that the clinical efficacy of a nano-enabled formulation does not automatically quantify the incremental benefit from its nanoscale component, and that translational value depends on factors such as antigen suitability, immune function, durability, and formulation-specific safety.
Controversies and gaps
There is evidence that the performance of complete vaccine formulations cannot be solely attributed to their nanoscale components. Additionally, the review notes that evidence from selected trial cohorts may not be generalizable across all Global South settings due to heterogeneity. The review also identifies gaps in understanding the links between platform function, clinical benefit, and feasible implementation.
Research and practice implications
The review emphasizes the need to assess manufacturing readiness, storage requirements, affordable complete schedules, regulatory capacity, and equitable access alongside biological performance. It also suggests that further research is required to establish the incremental benefits of nanoscale components in vaccine formulations and to improve generalizability across diverse populations in the Global South.

Impacto epidemiológico del dengue y del chikungunya en zonas Urbanas: revisión sistemática 城市地区登革热和基孔肯雅热的流行病学影响:系统评价

Impacto epidemiológico del dengue y del chikungunya en zonas urbanas: revisión sistemática Epidemiological impact of dengue and chikungunya in urban areas: a systematic review
作者： Nayeli Mishell Zamora-Villaprado, Thais Maité Silva-Saavedra, Mariam Mustafá Ibrahim-Santiago, Bolívar Fernando Mena-Hidalgo
摘要中文翻译目的:证明城市流行病学对登革热和基孔肯雅热的影响。方法:PubMed 和 SciELO 中的 síntesis narrativa 的修订(2012-2025);包括 15 个工作室。结果:1990 年世界登革热事件,拉丁美洲地区市场和货运方面存在差异。基孔肯雅热在 2013 年美洲地区得到扩展,并产生了城市爆炸性的产品,并具有持久的关节表现。城市化、埃及伊蚊和白纹伊蚊的扩张以及对传播有利于杀虫剂的抵抗力。Destacan como estrategias las nuevas vacunas y el control vectorial integrado。结论:虫媒病毒在热带和亚热带城市中发挥着重要作用,并在预防和控制方面发挥着重要作用。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
我们对系统进行了修订,以证明登革热和基孔肯雅热对城市流行病的影响。修订版 登革病毒 1–4 和登革热,不包括寨卡病毒、黄病毒、西尼罗病毒和日本脑炎病毒,这是登革热特有的证据。
证据基础与方法
2012 年至 2025 年 PubMed 和 SciELO 系统的修订,包括 15 个研究室。使用综合叙述来恢复证据,并实现元分析。
共识与结论
1990 年世界登革热疫情的结果,与拉丁美洲地区市场和其他地区的差异有关。基孔肯雅热在 2013 年美洲地区扩展,导致城市爆炸和关节持续表现。
争议与缺口
有证据表明,城市化、埃及伊蚊和白纹伊蚊的扩张,有助于抵抗杀虫剂,有利于安博斯病毒的传播。但禁止对城市背景下的 登革病毒 1-4 遗传变量的传输机制进行特殊研究。
科研与实践启示
Se destacan como estrategias las nuevas vacunas y el control vectorial integrado para mitigar el Impacto de la dengue.建议在 登革病毒 1-4 的城市地区进行流行病学警戒和医疗控制实施。

Impacto epidemiológico del dengue y del chikungunya en zonas urbanas: revisión sistemática Epidemiological impact of dengue and chikungunya in urban areas: a systematic review

Authors: Nayeli Mishell Zamora-Villaprado, Thais Maité Silva-Saavedra, Mariam Mustafá Ibrahim-Santiago, Bolívar Fernando Mena-Hidalgo
Original abstract (English)
Objetivo: sintetizar la evidencia sobre el impacto epidemiológico urbano del dengue y del chikungunya. Método: revisión sistemática con síntesis narrativa en PubMed y SciELO (2012-2025); se incluyeron 15 estudios. Resultados: la incidencia mundial del dengue aumentó desde 1990, con diferencias regionales marcadas y alta carga en América Latina. El chikungunya se expandió por las Américas desde 2013 y produjo brotes urbanos explosivos, con manifestaciones articulares persistentes. La urbanización, la expansión de Aedes aegypti y Aedes albopictus y la resistencia a insecticidas favorecieron la transmisión. Destacan como estrategias las nuevas vacunas y el control vectorial integrado. Conclusión: ambas arbovirosis son una carga creciente para las ciudades tropicales y subtropicales y exigen vigilancia y control sostenidos.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
El objetivo de esta revisión sistemática fue sintetizar la evidencia sobre el impacto epidemiológico urbano del dengue y del chikungunya. La revisión abarca Dengue viruses 1–4 y la enfermedad dengue, excluyendo otros flaviviruses como Zika, fiebre amarilla, virus del Nilo Occidental y virus de la encefalitis japonesa, a menos que se presenten evidencias específicas sobre el dengue.
Evidence base and method
La revisión sistemática se realizó en PubMed y SciELO entre 2012 y 2025, incluyendo 15 estudios. Se utilizó una síntesis narrativa para resumir la evidencia, sin realizar meta-análisis estadísticos.
Consensus and conclusions
Los resultados muestran que la incidencia mundial del dengue aumentó desde 1990, con diferencias regionales marcadas y una alta carga en América Latina. El chikungunya se expandió por las Américas desde 2013, causando brotes urbanos explosivos y manifestaciones articulares persistentes.
Controversies and gaps
La evidencia indica que la urbanización, la expansión de Aedes aegypti y Aedes albopictus, así como la resistencia a insecticidas, favorecen la transmisión de ambos virus. Sin embargo, no se reportan estudios que aborden de manera específica los mecanismos de transmisión o la variabilidad genética de los Dengue viruses 1–4 en contextos urbanos.
Research and practice implications
Se destacan como estrategias las nuevas vacunas y el control vectorial integrado para mitigar el impacto de la dengue. Se recomienda fortalecer la vigilancia epidemiológica y la implementación de medidas de control en áreas urbanas con alta prevalencia de los Dengue viruses 1–4.

Estrategias de prevención del dengue en comunidades Urbanas:revisión sistemática 城市社区登革热预防策略:系统回顾

Estrategias de prevención del dengue en comunidades urbanas: revisión sistemática Dengue prevention strategies in urban communities: a systematic review
作者： Angie Nicole Acosta-Obando, Gabriela Monica Echeverría-Chavez, Carlos Alejandro Troya-Altamirano
摘要中文翻译目的:证明在城市社区和厄瓜多尔环境中预防登革热的有效策略。方法:PubMed 和 SciELO 中的 síntesis narrativa 的修订(2012-2025);其中包括 15 个研究室:对系统的修订和元分析、厄瓜多尔的研究室、社区干预和经济评估。结果:控制力降低了昆虫学指标,从而影响了传播。El manejo 环境与社区参与、生活和受惠者的立方体最有利的结果。厄瓜多尔的水、水的感知和基本预防服务的故障。结论:城市登革热的预防需要综合、社区和支持、流行病学和基本服务的良好策略。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该审查旨在综合城市社区登革热预防策略有效性的证据,并评估其在厄瓜多尔国情中的适用性。
证据基础与方法
使用 PubMed 和 SciELO 数据库从 2012 年至 2025 年进行了系统评价。证据包括 15 项研究:有或没有荟萃分析的系统评价、在厄瓜多尔进行的研究、社区干预措施和经济评估。
共识与结论
审查发现,综合、基于社区和持续的策略,包括环境管理、纱窗和有盖水容器,在预防登革热方面显示出最有利的结果。然而,单独的化学控制降低了昆虫学指数,但没有显示出对 传播 的影响。
争议与缺口
审查强调,储水做法、风险认知和缺乏基本服务等因素影响了厄瓜多尔的预防有效性。没有明确提及结果中相互矛盾的发现或异质性。
科研与实践启示
城市地区的登革热预防需要综合、基于社区和持续的战略,以及流行病学 监测 和改善基本服务。

Estrategias de prevención del dengue en comunidades urbanas: revisión sistemática Dengue prevention strategies in urban communities: a systematic review

Authors: Angie Nicole Acosta-Obando, Gabriela Monica Echeverría-Chavez, Carlos Alejandro Troya-Altamirano
Original abstract (English)
Objetivo: sintetizar la evidencia sobre la efectividad de las estrategias de prevención del dengue en comunidades urbanas y su aplicabilidad al contexto ecuatoriano. Método: revisión sistemática con síntesis narrativa en PubMed y SciELO (2012-2025); se incluyeron 15 estudios: revisiones sistemáticas con y sin metaanálisis, estudios en Ecuador, intervenciones comunitarias y una evaluación económica. Resultados: el control químico aislado redujo los índices entomológicos sin efecto demostrado sobre la transmisión. El manejo ambiental con participación comunitaria, las mallas en las viviendas y las cubiertas de recipientes mostraron los resultados más favorables. El almacenamiento de agua, la percepción del riesgo y la falta de servicios básicos condicionaron la prevención en Ecuador. Conclusión: la prevención del dengue urbano requiere estrategias integradas, comunitarias y sostenidas, con vigilancia epidemiológica y mejoras de los servicios básicos.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review aims to synthesize evidence on the effectiveness of dengue prevention strategies in urban communities and assess their applicability to the Ecuadorian context.
Evidence base and method
A systematic review was conducted using PubMed and SciELO databases from 2012 to 2025. The evidence includes 15 studies: systematic reviews with and without meta-analyses, studies conducted in Ecuador, community interventions, and an economic evaluation.
Consensus and conclusions
The review found that integrated, community-based, and sustained strategies, including environmental management, window screens, and covered water containers, showed the most favorable results in dengue prevention. However, chemical control alone reduced entomological indices without demonstrated impact on transmission.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights that factors such as water storage practices, risk perception, and lack of basic services influenced prevention effectiveness in Ecuador. There is no explicit mention of conflicting findings or heterogeneity in the results.
Research and practice implications
The review concludes that dengue prevention in urban areas requires integrated, community-based, and sustained strategies, along with epidemiological surveillance and improvements in basic services.
